IV. kaṉṉaṭam



teluṅku moḻikku aṭuttapaṭiyāka iṭam peṟat takkatu kaṉṉaṭam. karnāṭakam eṉṟum, kāṉarīs eṉṟum atu vaḻaṅkap peṟum. maicūr, teṉ marāṭṭiya nāṭu, nijām nāṭṭu mēlaik kōṭṭaṅkaḷ cila, ākiya pakutikaḷil im moḻi vaḻaṅkivarukiṟatu. malapārk karaiyiluḷḷa kāṉarā eṉṉum kaṉṉaṭiyak kōṭṭattil, malaiyāḷam, tuḷu, koṅkaṇi ākiya moḻikaḷuṭaṉ kaṉṉaṭamum pēcappaṭukiṟatu. nīlakiriyiluḷḷa vaṭakar[1] eṉṉum perun tokai makkaḷ pēcuvatu paḻaiya kaṉṉaṭamē. im moḻi pēcuvōriṉ mottat tokai 1,05,00,000.

karnāṭam allatu karnāṭakam eṉṟa col mutaṟkaṇ teluṅku, kaṉṉaṭam eṉṟa iraṇṭu moḻikaḷaiyuṅ kuṟippataṟkē potuppaṭa vaḻaṅkap peṟṟatu; piṉṉar, teluṅkai nīkkik kaṉṉaṭattai maṭṭumē atu kuṟippatāyiṟṟu. karnāṭakam eṉpatu vaṭamoḻiccolliliruntu piṟantatu eṉpar vaṭamoḻip pulavar. āyiṉum, ṭākṭar kuṇṭerṭ kūṟuvatupōl karu+nāṭu+akam eṉṟa tamiḻc coṟkaḷiṉ aṭiyākap piṟantatu ac col eṉṟu koḷvatē ciṟappākum.

tamiḻiṟ centamiḻ, koṭun tamiḻ eṉṟa iru pirivukaḷ iruppaṉa pōṉṟu kaṉṉaṭattilum, paḻaiya kaṉṉaṭam eṉṟum, putuk kaṉṉaṭam eṉṟum iru pirivukaḷ uḷḷaṉa. ip pirivukaḷukkiṭaiyēyuḷḷa vēṟupāṭu tamiḻaiyum malaiyāḷattaiyum pōṉṟu vaṭamoḻic coṟkalappiṉāl ēṟpaṭṭa toṉṟaṉṟu; collākka muṭipukaḷiṉālēyē ēṟpaṭṭa toṉṟākum. paḻaiya kaṉṉaṭam eṉṟa moḻiyum, paḻaiya kaṉṉaṭam eṉṟa varivaṭivamum oṉṟaṉṟu. atu vēṟu, itu vēṟēyām. iraṇṭaiyum oṉṟuṭaṉoṉṟu kalattal kūṭātu. maicūrilum, marāṭṭiya nāṭṭilum kāṇappaṭum kalveṭṭukaḷiṟ pala paḻaiya kaṉṉaṭam eṉṉum varivaṭivattā liyaṉṟaṉavēyām. haௗ kaṉṉaṭam eṉṟa paḻaiya kaṉṉaṭattiluḷḷa kalveṭṭukkaḷiṉ coṟkaḷellām vaṭamoḻiyē yaṉṟik kaṉṉaṭamalla.

karnāṭakam eṉṟa col paṉṉūṟāṇṭukaḷākavē vaḻakkil iruntuvarukiṟatu. ki. pi. aintām nūṟṟāṇṭiṉarāṉa varāhamihirar ataṉai eṭuttāḷukiṟār. tāranātarum karnāṭam eṉpataik kuṟikkiṟār. mukammatiyarkaḷ itaṉaik karnāṭṭik eṉṟaṉar. āṅkilēyarkaḷ “kāṉarīs” eṉṟu tirittu vaḻaṅkalāyiṉar.



↑ Badagas





V. tuḷu



tuḷu allatu tuḷuvam tiruntiyatoru moḻiyēyākum. eṉiṉum, ataṟkut taṉippaṭṭa varivaṭivamō, ilakkiyamō illāmaiyāl ataṉait tiruntiya moḻiyiṉattil cērttal ollumō eṉṟu aiyuṟutal kūṭum. tuḷu moḻiyil mutaṉmutalāka nūlkaḷ cila acciṭṭavarkaḷ pēcil miṣaṉaic[1] cērnta kurumārkaḷē yāvar. āṉāl avarkaḷ an nūlkaḷaik kaṉṉaṭa moḻiyiṉ varivaṭivilēyē acciṭṭuviṭṭaṉar. ātalāl, tuḷu moḻikkuriya eḻuttu kaṉṉaṭamē eṉṟu nampappaṭuvatāyiṟṟu. ilakkiyamillaiyēṉum tuḷu mikavum tiruntiya tirāviṭa moḻikaḷuḷoṉṟu eṉṟu tāṉ kūṟavēṇṭum.

im moḻi ciṟu tokaiyiṉarāṉa makkaḷāl kuṟukiya aḷavuḷḷa oru pakutiyilmaṭṭum pēcappaṭṭu varukiṟatu. kaṉṉaṭak kōṭṭattil oṭum cantirakiri, kalyāṇapuri eṉṟa iraṇṭu āṟukaḷum im moḻi vaḻaṅkum pakutiyiṉ iraṇṭu ellaikaḷākak karutappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. av vellaikaḷaik kaṭantu atu vaḻaṅkiyatākak kūṟutaṟku millai.

tuḷu moḻi pēcuvōriṉ tokai 5,00,000-kku mēṟpaṭṭatākum. iruntālum avariṭaiyē pala moḻikaḷ vaḻaṅkukiṉṟaṉa. ātalāl tuḷu eṅkē oruvaḻiyāy maṟaintoḻintuviṭumō eṉṟukūṭa aiyuṟalām. iruntālum atu maṟaintoḻitaṟkāṉa kuṟikaḷ oṉṟum ituvaraik kāṇappaṭṭilatu. tirāviṭa makkaḷukkuḷ māṟutal eṉpatē vēṇṭāta ōr iṉattār irukkakkūṭu meṉṟāl avarkaḷ tuḷuvarkaḷē. ātaliṉ avarkaḷ tam moḻiyai maṟaintoḻiyavum viṭuvarō!

tuḷu eṉpatu paṇivu, aṭakkam eṉṟu poruḷpaṭum eṉa pirikel[2] eṉṟa kaṉṉaṭa ilakkaṇa nūlāciriyar kūṟu kiṟār. itiliruntu accol am moḻi pēcum makkaḷaiyē yaṉṟi am moḻiyaik kuṟittatākak koḷḷa iṭamillai.

malaiyāḷa moḻiyiṉ kiḷai moḻiyē tuḷu eṉṟu ellis[3] nampiṉār. avar avvāṟu nampiyataṟku malaiyāḷa moḻi arukiṟ peru vaḻakki liruppatum, tuḷuvap pārppaṉarkaḷ vaṭamoḻiyil eḻuta malaiyāḷa eḻuttukkaḷaiyē perumpālum payaṉpaṭutti varuvatum kāraṇaṅkaḷākalām. ik kāraṇaṅkaḷ cālā. tuḷuvam malaiyāḷattiṉ kiḷaiyaṉ ṟeṉṟu uṟutiyākak kūṟalām. "tuḷu ilakkaṇam" vakutta pirikel itaik kuṟittup pala uṇmaikaḷaiyeṭuttuk kūṟi viḷakkiyuḷḷār. malaiyāḷattiṟkum tamiḻukkum irukkum vēṟupāṭṭaiviṭa mikunta vēṟupāṭu tuḷuvattiṟkum malaiyāḷattiṟku miṭaiyē uḷḷatu. āṉāl ataṟkum kaṉṉaṭa moḻikku miṭaiyē at tuṇai vēṟupāṭillai. kuṭaku moḻikkum tuḷuvattiṟku miṭaiyiluḷḷa vēṟupāṭō mikamikak kuṟaivu. tamiḻ moḻiyiliruntō kuṭaku peritum vēṟupaṭukiṟatu.

ceṉṉaik karukil vacittuvanta kuṟumparkaḷ eṉṟa nāṭōṭi iṭaiyarkaḷ turattappaṭṭu avarkaḷuṭaiya nila pulaṅkaḷ tuḷuva nāṭṭu vēḷāḷarkaḷukku vaḻaṅkap peṟṟaṉa eṉṟa oru paḻaṅ kataiyai ellis kūṟiyuḷḷār. ceṉṉaiyaiyum, ceṉṉaiyaiyaṭuttu muḷḷa cila kuṭumpattiṉar taṅkaḷait tuḷuva vēḷāḷar eṉṟu aḻaittukkoḷvatu mēṟkuṟitta paḻaṅ kataik koḷkaiyai valiyuṟuttuvatākum. iruntālum, tuḷuva nāṭṭiliruntu palar ceṉṉaikku vantu kuṭiyēṟi yirukkakkūṭum eṉṟu koḷvataṟkillai ; eṉeṉil, avvāṟiruntāl ceṉṉai nakarat tamiḻ ciṟcila tuṟaikaḷilēṉum tuḷuva moḻip paṇpukaḷai ēṟṟukkoṇṭiruttal iyalpākumātaliṉ. avvāṟu tuḷuva moḻik kalappu iruppatākat teriyavillai. ceṉṉai nakarat tamiḻ, ceṉṉai maṇṭilat tamiḻiliruntu vēṟupaṭṭuk kāṇpatu teluṅku moḻik kalappiṉālēyē yaṉṟit tuḷumoḻik kalappiṉālaṉṟu.



↑ Basle Missionaries

↑ Mr. Brigel

↑ Mr. Ellis





VI. kuṭaku



kuṭakukku ilakkiyam illai. tuḷuvai oppakkūṭa itaṉaip paṇpaṭṭa moḻi eṉṟu colla muṭiyātu. eṉavē, mutaṟkaṇ itaṉait taṉi moḻiyākak kaṇakkiṭāmal kaṉṉaṭattuḷ aṭakkik kūṟappaṭṭuvantatu. itaṉ uṟavaip paṟṟiya ārāycci iṉṉum teḷivu peṟavillai. ṭākṭar mōkliṅ[1] eṉṟa jermāṉiya ārāycciyāḷar itu kaṉṉaṭattaiviṭat tamiḻ malaiyāḷam ivaṟṟukku aṇmaiyāṉatu eṉṟu kūṟiṉar. tirāviṭak kuḻuvil iḵtu etaṉuṭaṉ nerukkamāṉa uṟavuṭaiya teṉṟu teḷivākat teriyavillai yāyiṉum, mēṟpaṭaiyāka nōkkiṉ, itu paḻaṅ kaṉṉaṭattiṟkum tuḷuviṟkum iṭaippaṭṭa teṉat tōṟṟukiṟatu. im moḻiyiṉ ilakkaṇa moṉṟum cila pāṭṭukkaḷum mējar kōl[2] eṉpavarāl veḷiyiṭappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa.

“nīlakiriyiluḷḷa paṇṭaik kuṭikaḷiṉ moḻikaḷaip pōṉṟu im moḻiyum (kuṭaku) taṉ toṉmaip paṇpu māṟāmal iruntu varukiṟateṉpatil aiyamillai. iḵtu im makkaḷ perumpālum otuṅki vāḻntu varuvataṉālēyē yākum. kuṭaku makkaḷ paṇṭaik kālattilēyē mēṟku malaittoṭariṟ kuṭiyēṟiviṭṭaṉar eṉpatu avarkaḷiṭaiyē iṉṟum oru peṇ pala āṭavarkaḷai maṇantu koḷḷum vaḻakkamiruntuvaruvataṉāṟ pulappaṭum. iv vaḻakkam paṇṭait tirāviṭar vaḻakkamākum. ivarkaḷukku ilakkiyamillai; pārppaṉar paḻakka vaḻakkaṅkaḷ iṉṟaḷavum ivarkaḷiṭaip pukavillai” eṉṟu parṉel[3] eḻutiyuḷḷār.

itukāṟum viritturaitta āṟu moḻikaḷum tirāviṭak kuḻuviṉuḷ tiruntiyavaiyākak koḷḷak kiṭappaṉa. iṉik kūṟappōkum āṟum, ivaṟṟiṟku māṟākat, tiruntātaṉa; atāvatu eḻuttum ilakkiyamum illātaṉa.



↑ Dr. Mogling

↑ Major Cole

↑ Burnell's “Specimens of South Indian Dialects: No. 3."





tiruntā moḻikaḷ



(I) tutam :

tutam allatu totam eṉpatu nīlakiri malaiyiluṟaiyum tutavar allatu totavariṉ moḻiyām. ivarkaḷ tokai ekkālattum oṉṟi raṇ ṭāyirattiṟku mikutiyā yiruntirukka muṭiyātu. apiṉip paḻakkattālum, peṇkaḷ pala kaṇavarkaḷaik koḷḷum muṟaiyālum, peṇ kuḻantaikaḷaip piṟantavuṭaṉ koṉṟuviṭum vaḻakkam oru kālattil kaiyāḷappaṭ ṭiruntamaiyālum ivarkaḷ tokaiyil aruki, iṉṟu 700 aḷavil kuṟaintirukkiṉṟaṉar. āyiṉum, ivarkaḷ moḻi oppiyal moḻiyilak kaṇattiṟkup payaṉpaṭum ciṟappukkaḷ uṭaiyatu.

tuta allatu tota eṉpatu tamiḻt tōḻaṉ (kūṭṭattāṉ) eṉpatuṭaṉ uṟavuṭaiyatu eṉṟu ṭākṭar pōp koṇṭaṉar. āṉāl, aḵtu oppukkoḷḷat takkataṉṟu.

(II) kōtam :

totavarkaḷē yaṉṟi, nīlakiri malaiyil kōtarkaḷ eṉṟa oru ciṟukuṭiyiṉarum vāḻntuvarukiṉṟaṉar. ivarkaḷ perumpālum kūlit toḻilāḷikaḷē yāvar. ivarkaḷiṉ tokai ēṟakkuṟaiya 1200 ākum. paṇṭai nāṭkaḷilirunta uyarkuṭi makkaḷāl turattappaṭṭu ivarkaḷ iṅkē vantu kuṭiyēṟiyavarka ḷātalvēṇṭum. ivarkaḷ moḻiyākiya kōtam kaṉṉaṭattiṉ koccaikkiḷai eṉṟu oruvāṟu kūṟalām. 1911-ām āṇṭu eṭutta makkaṭṭokaik kaṇakkiṉpaṭi assām nāṭṭil oṉpatiṉmar kōtar iruntārkaḷām.

iṉi, it totavamum kōtamumēyaṉṟi, vaṭakarkaḷ pēcum oru taṉippaṭṭa moḻiyum, iruḷarkaḷ pēcum tamiḻ moḻic citaivum, kuṟumparkaḷ eṉṟa mullai nila makkaḷ pēcum tamiḻmoḻic citaivum nīlakiri malait toṭaril vaḻaṅkukiṉṟaṉa.

(III) kōṇṭu :

kōṇṭarkaḷ mattiya intiyāviluḷḷa kuṉṟukaḷilum kāṭukaḷilum uṟaiyum paḻaṅkuṭikaḷ āvar. ivarkaḷatu tokai patiṉaintu nūṟāyiram (15,00,000) ākum.[1] ivarkaḷ vāḻum pakutiyai muṉṉaiya ārāycciyāḷar kōṇṭavaṉam eṉṟaḻaittaṉar. kōṇṭarkaḷ tāmē tammaik kuṟikka vaḻaṅkiya peyar kōyītōr[2] atāvatu kōyīkkaḷ eṉpatākum. ivariṭaiyē 12 vakuppukkaḷ uḷḷaṉa. avaṟṟuḷ 4 vakuppiṉar tammaic ciṟappākak kōyītōr eṉṟu kūṟikkoḷḷukiṉṟaṉar. ivaruḷ oru vakuppiṉar kōhitūr eṉṟu tammaik kuṟikkum māriyār āvar. ivarkaḷē kōṇṭar aṉaivarilum vīramum muraṭṭut taṉamum mikkavar. kalappaṟṟa paḻaṅkōṇṭarkaḷum ivarkaḷē yāvar eṉṟu eṇṇa iṭamuṇṭu.

(IV) kantam allatu ku :

āṅkila aṟiñar ivarkaḷaik kontar eṉṟu vaḻaṅkukiṉṟaṉar. āṉāl ivarkaḷai aṭuttuḷḷōr ivarkaḷaik kantar eṉṟum, ivarkaḷ tāmē tammaik ku eṉṟum vaḻaṅkukiṉṟaṉar. ivarkaḷ kōṇṭavaṉattiṉ kiḻakkup pakkattilum, orissāviṉ kuṉṟukaḷilum uṟaipavarkaḷ. kōṇṭaruṭaṉ uṟavuṭaiyavar ivar eṉṟu karutappaṭukiṟatu. kōṇṭar eṉṟa peyarum, kantar eṉṟa ivar peyaruṅkūṭak kuṉṟu eṉṟa tamiḻccol allatu ataṟkuc cariyāṉa koṇṭa eṉṟa teluṅkuc colliliruntu vantateṉac cilar collukiṉṟaṉar. āṉāl, itaṟku ovvāta muṟaiyil ivarkaḷai yaṭuttu vāḻum teluṅkarē ivarkaḷaik kontar eṉṟum kōtar eṉṟum aḻaikkiṉṟaṉar.

kaḻinta (1911) kaṇakkuppaṭi ivarkaḷatu tokai aintu nūṟāyirattu muppatiṉāyiram (5,30,000) ākum.

(V) irājmahāl :

mālarkaḷ eṉṟum, irājmahālar eṉṟum ivarkaḷ peyarpeṟuvar. vaṅkāḷakkiluḷḷa irājmahāl malaikaḷil ivarkaḷ paṇṭaikkālattiliruntu vāḻntu varupavarkaḷ. irājhāl eṉpatu pāṟaiyar allatu kuṉṟa makkaḷ eṉṟu poruḷpaṭum. im moḻiyiṉ aṭippaṭai tirāviṭa iṉaktaic cērntatē eṉpatu “āciya moḻiyārāyccikaḷ"[3] eṉṟa nūliṉ aintān tokutiyil eṭuttuk koṭukkappaṭṭuḷḷa irājmahāl moḻic coṟkaḷ cilavaṟṟiliruntum, hāṭjsaṉ eṉpār eḻutiya “vaṅkāḷa makkaḷiṉ varalāṟu"[4] eṉṉum nūliṟ kāṇappaṭum coṟpaṭṭiyiliruntum naṉku viḷaṅkum. cantāḷar eṉ pōriṉ moḻiyōṭu itaṉaik kalantuviṭutal kūṭātu. im moḻiyiṉ ilakkaṇa amaippaippaṟṟi oṉṟun terivataṟkillai. im makkaḷiṉ tokai ēṟakkuṟaiya 64,000.



↑ kōṇṭarkaḷ paḻaṅkuṭi makkaḷ; ākaiyāl ikkālattut tiruntiya camūka vāḻkkaiyil iṭam peṟak kūṭātavar eṉṟu tāḻtti vaikkappaṭṭirunta nilaimaiyai rīvāp pēraracar nīkki, kōṇṭarkaḷ iṉi kṣattiriyarkaḷākavē pārāṭṭappaṭṭu, kṣattiriyarkaḷukkuriya ellā urimaikaḷaiyum peṟuvar eṉṟu kaṭṭaḷai piṟappittuḷḷār- (asōsi yēṭṭeṭ piras) - intiyaṉ ekspiras (19-2-41)



↑ Koitor

↑ The Asiatic Researches.

↑ Ethnology of Bengal-Col. Dalton.





(VI) ōrāvōṉ :

cūṭiya nākapuriyilum, ataic cuṟṟilum vāḻum ōrāvōṉ iṉattāriṉ tokai 8,00,000. “vaṅkāḷa makkaḷiṉ varalāṟu”[1] eṉṟa nūlil karṉal ṭālṭaṉ ivarkaḷaip paṟṟiya cuvaiyāṉa varalāṟṟuk kuṟippoṉṟu eḻutiyuḷḷār. ivarkaḷ moḻiyum tirāviṭa moḻiyaiyē aṭippaṭaiyāyuṭaiyatākum. pāṭsk pātiriyār im moḻikkuriya ilakkaṇac curukkam[2] oṉṟu eḻutiyuḷḷār.

ivarkaḷ taṅkaḷaik kurṅkarkaḷ[3] eṉṟu kūṟikkoḷkiṉṟaṉar. koṅkaṇattiliruntu vantatākavum, rōtā[4] malaikaḷilum pāṭalipurak kōṭṭattai yaṭutta malaikaḷilum neṭunāḷ vāḻntiruntatākavum, aṅkiruntu turattappaṭṭa poḻutu irājmahāl malaikaḷukku oru pakutiyiṉarum, cūṭiya nākapuri malaikaḷukku maṟṟoru pakutiyiṉarumākac ceṉṟu kuṭiyēṟiyatākavum avarkaḷ kūṟikkoḷkiṉṟaṉar. moḻi yoṟṟumaikaḷum, paḻakka vaḻakka voṟṟumaikaḷum itaṉai valiyuṟuttukiṉṟaṉa. mūṇṭarkaḷum kōlēriyarkaḷum vantu kuṭiyēṟuvataṟku muṉṉarē orāvōṉiyarkaḷ cūṭiya nākapuriyil vantuviṭṭārkaḷ eṉpatu vaḻakkāṟu.

tutam, kōtam, kōṇṭu mutaliya moḻikaḷ tiruttamaṟṟavaiyā yiruntapōtilum, tamiḻ, teluṅku, kaṉṉaṭam mutaliya tiruntiya moḻikaḷaip pōlavē avai tūya tirāviṭa moḻikaḷ eṉpatil aiyamillai. avaṟṟiṟ kaṭuttapaṭiyāka, irājmahāl, orāvōṉ eṉṟa iraṇṭu moḻikaḷaiyum tirāviṭa moḻikaḷākak koḷḷalām. ēṉeṉil, ivaṟṟil cuṭṭup peyarkaḷ, mutal nāṉku eṇkaḷ pōṉṟa iṉṟiyamaiyāc coṟkaḷai uḷḷiṭṭa mutaṟ coṟkaḷil perumpālāṉavai tirāviṭac coṟkaḷē yāyiṉum avaṟṟōṭukūṭap piṟa kuḻuc coṟkaḷum kalantuḷḷaṉa. ip piṟakuḻu kōlēriyak kuḻu eṉak koḷḷappaṭukiṟatu. eṉavē, ivviru moḻikaḷum tirāviṭa moḻikaḷēyāyiṉum piṟa tirāviṭa moḻikaḷaiviṭat tūymai kuṟaintavaiyē. ivaṟṟuḷḷum, orāvōṉ irājmahālaiyum, kōlēriya iṉattaiyum iṇaippateṉṟum, irājmahāl atēpōṉṟu orāvōṉaiyum tirāviṭa moḻikaḷaiyum iṇaippa teṉṟum hāṭjsaṉ koḷkiṟār. eppaṭiyum ōrāvōṉaiviṭa irājmahālē tirāviṭac cārpu mikutiyu muṭaiyateṉpatu tēṟṟam.

mattiya maṇṭilattilum, vaṅkāḷattilum vaḻaṅkum tiruntā moḻikaḷuḷ iraṇṭilāvatu tirāviṭat toṭarpu irukkiṉṟatu eṉpatu mēṟkūṟip pōntavaṟṟāl iṉitu viḷaṅkum. eṉavē, tirāviṭap peruṅ kuḻuviṉar kaṅkaik karaiyi lillāviṭṭālum, vaṅkāḷa maṇṭilam varaiyilum paravi yiruntārkaḷ eṉpatu pōtarukiṉṟatu. muṉṉaip paḻaṅkālattil tirāviṭak kuḻuviṉar intiyā muḻutumē paraviyiruntiruttal vēṇṭum eṉṟa koḷkai oruvāṟu itaṉāl valiyuṟuttappaṭukiṉṟatu.

karṉal ṭālṭaṉ tammuṭaiya ”vaṅkāḷa makkaḷ varalāṟu” eṉṟa nūlil tirāviṭat toṭarpaik kuṟittuk kūṟiyuḷḷa pakuti varumāṟu:

"potuvāka ippoḻutu nampappaṭṭu varuvataik kāṭṭilum mikap peruvāriyākak tirāviṭat toṭarpu vaṅkāḷa makkaḷiṭai kāṇappaṭukiṟatu. iṉṟu intup paḻakka vaḻakkaṅkaḷai mēṟkoṇṭavarkaḷākak kāṇappaṭum vaṅkāḷap paḻaṅkuṭikaḷiṟ perumpakutiyiṉar tirāviṭakkuḻuvaic cērntavarkaḷē. vaṅkāḷak kōṭṭaṅkaḷilum, cūṭiya nākapuri, orisāp pakutikaḷilum avaṟṟai yaṭutta kāṭukaḷilum vāḻntuvarum pūyiyarkaḷ[5] aṉaivarum tirāviṭarkaḷē; koccarkaḷ[6] eṉṟa iṉattārum tirāviṭarkaḷē. pūyiyarkaḷiṉ tokai ēṟakkuṟaiya 25,00,000; koccarkaḷiṉ tokai 15,00,000. inta nāṟpatu nūṟāyiravarum vaṅkāḷa maṇṭila makkaṭṭokaiyil pattil oru paṅkiṉarāvar. ivarkaḷ tirāviṭa iṉattāruḷ cērkkappaṭa vēṇṭiyavarkaḷē.” im muṭipu ārāyattakka toṉṟākum.

koḷkai vēṟṟumaik kiṭamuḷḷa ittakaiya pakutikaḷai nīkki, aiyattiṟkē iṭamiṉṟit tirāviṭar eṉa ēṟṟukkoḷḷattakka vakuppukaḷum avaṟṟiṉ tokaikaḷum 1911-m āṇṭu makkaḷ tokaik kaṇakkiṉpaṭi kīḻē tarappaṭukiṉṟaṉa.

tirāviṭa moḻikaḷ pēcupavar tokai

1. tamiḻ 1,91,89,740[7]



2. teluṅku 2,35,42,859



3. kaṉṉaṭam 1,05,25,739



4. malaiyāḷam 67,92,277



5. tuḷu 5,31,498



6. kuṭaku 42,881



7. tuṭam 730



8. kōtam 1,280



9. kōṇṭu 15,27,157



10. kantam (ku) 5,30,476



11. irājmahāl 64,875



12. orāvōṉ 8,00,328



mottat tirāviṭar

6,35,49,846



ik kaṇakkiṉpaṭi tirāviṭamoḻi pēcuvōriṉ mottat tokai 6 kōṭiyē 40 nūṟāyiram ātal kāṇka. ik kaṇakkil nāṭōṭikaḷāṉa irāmūsikaḷ, ilampāṭikaḷ mutaliyavarkaḷaic cērkkavillai. ēṉeṉil, ilampāṭikaḷ pēcuvatu oruvakai intustāṉiyēyākum. irāmūsikaḷ moḻiyō teluṅkiṉ marūu. ātalāl, avai taṉiyākak kūṟappaṭa vēṇṭiyavaiyalla. mēṟkuttoṭariṉ teṉ pakutiyil vāḻum malaiyaracarkaḷilō caṟṟu vaṭakkil malaiyāḷa moḻi vaḻaṅkum pakutiyiṉ aṇmaiyiluḷḷōr malaiyāḷac citaivumoḻi oṉṟaiyum, teṟkē tamiḻ moḻi vaḻaṅkum pakutiyiṉ aṇmaiyiluḷḷōr malaiyāḷak kalappuṟṟa tamiḻc citaivumoḻi oṉṟaiyumē pēcukiṉṟaṉar.

iṉi, mattiya intiyāvilum vaṅkattilum uḷḷa hō, muṇṭā mutaliya kōlēriya moḻikaḷum, cavararkaḷiṉ moḻikaḷum tirāviṭa moḻikaḷuḷ cērkkappaṭa villai. ivaṟṟai car jārj kāmpel kōlēriya iṉameṉṟum hāṭjsaṉ tamiḻiṉam eṉṟum vakuttuḷḷārkaḷ. kōṇṭu, ku, irājmahāl, orāvōṉ mutiliya moḻikaḷuṭaṉ ivai uṟavuṭaiyavaiyākat tōṟṟukiṉṟaṉa eṉpatu uṇmaiyē. āṉāl ivvuṟavu orucila tirāviṭa mutaṟcoṟkaḷuṭaṉ niṉṟuviṭukiṟatu. ilakkaṇa amaippil yātōr oṟṟumaiyumillai. ittakaiya uṟavu iṉa uṟavākamāṭṭātu; neṭunāḷaiya iṭa aṇmai yaiyum paḻakkattaiyumē kāṭṭuvatākum.

intiyāviṉ vaṭakiḻakkil pōṭōvar[8] timālar[9] mutaliyavar moḻikaḷaiyum, kumāvōṉ[10] asām ivaṟṟiṭaiyēyuḷḷa malaikkāṭṭu makkaḷiṉ moḻikaḷaiyum ivvāṟē tirāviṭa moḻiyiṉattiṟ cērkkavillai. hāṭjsaṉ ivaṟṟaiyum tamiḻkkuḻu eṉṟē matittaṉar. itaṟkuk kāraṇam avar āriya iṉamallā moḻikaḷ ellāvaṟṟaiyum orē kuḻu eṉṟum, atil talaimaiyāṉa moḻi tamiḻ eṉṟum koṇṭatē yākum. āṉāl ilakkaṇa amaippu, mutaṟcol tokuti ivaṟṟai ārāyntu pārttāl avai tirāviṭa moḻikaḷuṭaṉ attakaiya nērāṉa iṉa oṟṟumai uṭaiyavaiyākat tōṟṟa villai. avar kāṭṭiya ciṟcila ilakkaṇa amaippu oppumaikaḷ im moḻikaḷukkumaṭṭumēyaṉṟic cittiya iṉa moḻikaḷ aṉaittiṟkum potuppaṭṭavaiyām. ātalāl avaṟṟait tirāviṭak kuḻuvuṭaṉ cērppatu turukkiyak kuḻuvuṭaṉ cērppatai yoppatēyākum eṉka.

palūccistāṉattil kelattut tokutiyil pēcappaṭum pirākuvī moḻi tirāviṭac coṟkaḷ maṭṭu maṉṟit tirāviṭa ilakkaṇa amaippukkūṭa uṭaiyatātalāl, atu hāṭjsaṉ tamiḻkkuḻuvil cērtta nēpāḷa, pūtāṉat tamiḻmoḻikaḷaiviṭa, tirāviṭak kuḻuvuṭaṉ cērkkappaṭuvataṟku ettaṉaiyō maṭaṅku urimaiyuṭaiya tēyākum. āṉāl atiluṅkūṭat tirāviṭap pakuti kuṟaivākavum, piṟa pakuti mikutiyākavum iruppatāl, tirāviṭa oppiyal ilakkaṇattiṟku utavum iṭaṅkaḷilmaṭṭum aḵtu eṭuttukkāṭṭappaṭum.

1911-m āṇṭuk kaṇakkil pirākuvī tirāviṭa moḻikaḷuḷ vaittu eṇṇappaṭṭuḷḷatu. ak kaṇakkiṉpaṭi pēcuvōr tokai 170,998 ākum. itu tavira 236 pēr pēcum malhar[11] eṉpatoṉṟum, 24,074 pēr pēcum kolāmī[12] eṉpatoṉṟum tirāviṭa iṉattuḷ cērkkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. pirākuvī moḻiyil orucila talaimaiyāṉa coṟkaḷ tirāviṭac cārpuṭaiyavaiyāyiṉum, coṟkaḷuḷ perumpālāṉa tirāviṭattiṉ vēṟāyirukkiṉṟaṉa. āṉāl ilakkaṇa amaippilō teḷivāṉa tirāviṭa oṟṟumaikaḷ uḷḷaṉa. pirāruvī makkaḷ tāṅkaḷ hāleppi[13] (alep pōvi) liruntu vantatākak kūṟukiṉṟaṉar. iḵtu orukāl avarkaḷiṭaiyē piṉvanta oru kūṭṭattāraik kuṟippatāyirukka vēṇṭum. aḵtu āriyak kūṭṭamākavum iruntirukkakkūṭum. āṉāl, eppaṭiyum tirāviṭac cārpāṉa oru mutal kūṭṭam iruntirukka vēṇṭum. ataippaṟṟi ittirāviṭa oṟṟumaikaḷāl terivatu tavira vēṟetuvum teriyavillai. piṉvanta kūṭṭattār ivarkaḷai veṉṟu ivarkaḷuṭaṉ kalantirukkalām.

pirākuvī moḻi tirāviṭa vakuppaic cintu āṟu kaṭantu naṭu āciyāvaraiyiṟ koṇṭupōyc cērkkiṉṟatu. ammoḻi uṇmaiyil pañcāpi, cinti ivaṟṟuṭaṉ otta moḻiyāyiṉum tirāviṭap pakuti oṉṟuṭaṉ iṇaintuḷḷa teṉpatu maṟukkakkūṭāta uṇmaiyākum. it tirāviṭap pakutiyai nōkkat tirāviṭarum piṉṉāḷaiya āriyar, kirēkkar, cittiyar, maṅkōliyar mutaliyavarkaḷaippōl vaṭa mēṟkiliruntu vantavartāmō eṉṟu eṇṇa iṭamirukkiṉṟatu.



* * *

﻿4. tirāviṭa marapu moḻikaḷ orē mūla moḻiyiṉ maṇṭilavakaittiripu moḻikaḷ alla



orē maṇṭilattil vaḻaṅkum moḻikaḷ kōṭṭattiṟkuk kōṭṭam ciṟcila māṟutalkaḷuṭaṉum, ciṟappiyal pukaḷuṭaṉum vaḻaṅkappaṭutalkūṭum. appoḻutu avaṟṟait ticaimoḻikaḷ eṉṟum, vakaittiripumoḻikaḷ eṉṟum koḷvatu vaḻakkāṟu. av vaḻakkāṟṟiṉpaṭi mēṟkuṟikkappaṭṭa paṉṉiraṇṭu vakai moḻikaḷaiyum orē mūla tirāviṭa moḻiyiṉ vakait tiripumoḻikaḷ eṉṟu koḷvataṟkillai. ēṉeṉil, ivaṟṟiṉiṭaiyē uḷḷa vēṟṟumai yiyalpukaḷ palappala vātaliṉālēyē. oru vakaiyiṉar pēcum moḻiyai maṟṟoruvakaiyiṉar eḷitil kēṭṭup poruḷuṇarntukoḷḷa muṭiyātu. tamiḻaiyum, malaiyāḷattaiyum ēṟakkuṟaiya neruṅkiya toṭarpuṭaiyaṉa vākak koḷḷalām. eṉiṉum, avvam moḻiyil pēcappaṭum eḷiya ciṟu toṭarkaḷaiyē maṟṟa moḻiyāḷar uṇarntukoḷḷutal iyalum. aṭukkut toṭarkaḷaiyō, cikkalāṉa vākkiyaṅkaḷaiyō oruvarukkoruvar tattam moḻiyiṟ kūṟiṉal maṟṟavar aṟintukoḷḷutal iyalātu.

pēcum moḻi orupuṟamirukka, ilakkiyattai eṭuttukkoṇṭālum itē vēṟupāṭutāṉ. tamiḻ, malaiyāḷam, teluṅku, kaṉṉaṭam ākiya nāṉku moḻikaḷil ovvoṉṟiṟkum taṉippaṭṭa ciṟappāṉa ilakkiyak kalaivaḷam irukkiṉṟatu. tamiḻ, malaiyāḷam, teluṅku ākiya mūṉṟil ovvoṉṟukkum taṉippaṭṭa vari vaṭivamum uḷḷatu. āṉal, ikkālai vaḻaṅkum kaṉṉaṭa eḻuttukkaḷō teluṅkiliruntu peṟappaṭṭu, oruciṟitu māṟupaṭṭiyalukiṉṟaṉa. ik kāraṇattāl kaṉṉaṭam, teluṅkuṭaṉ neruṅkiya uṟavuṭaiyatu eṉṟu koḷvataṟku millai. ēṉ? moḻi eṉṟa muṟaiyil, kaṉṉaṭa moḻi teluṅkaiviṭat tamiḻaiyē peritum cārntatākum. atilum, paḻaiya kaṉṉaṭa eḻuttukkaḷ teluṅkeḻuttukkaḷukku muṟṟilum vēṟāṉavaiyām.

tiruntiya moḻikaḷākak koḷḷappaṭṭa, tamiḻ, teluṅku, kaṉṉaṭam, malaiyāḷam, tuḷu, kuṭaku eṉṟa āṟaṉuḷ, tamiḻiṟkum teluṅkiṟkum iṭaiyēyuḷḷa vēṟupāṭu mikap peritām. iru moḻikaḷilum uḷḷa vērc coṟkaḷiṟ perumpālaṉa iraṇṭaṟkum potuvākak kāṇappaṭukiṉṟamai uṇmaiyē. eṉiṉum, tamiḻai maṭṭum aṟinta oruvar, teluṅkil ilakkaṇa muṟaippaṭi amaikkappaṭṭa oru muḻu nīṇṭa coṟṟoṭarai aṟintukoḷḷutal eṉpatu eḷitil muṭiyātu; avvāṟē teluṅkaimaṭṭum aṟinta oruvar tamiḻil ilakkaṇa muṟaippaṭi amaikkappaṭṭa oru muḻu nīṇṭa coṟṟoṭarai aṟintukoḷḷutal eṉpatum eḷitil muṭiyātu. eṉavē, im moḻikaḷ aṉaittum orē mūla moḻiyiṉiṉṟum kiḷaittaṉavē yāyiṉum, iṉṟaiya nilaiyil, oṉṟaṟkoṉṟu uṟavuṭaiya taṉippaṭṭa moḻikaḷākavē karutappaṭum.

tiruntiya im moḻikaḷiṉiṭaiyē ittuṇai vēṟu pāṭāṉal, tiruntā moḻikaḷāṉa tutam, kōtam, kōṇṭu, kantam, orāvōṉ eṉṟa moḻikaḷukkiṭaiyē mikuntu kāṇappaṭum vēṟṟumaikaḷai eṭutturaikkavum vēṇṭumō? uṇmaiyaik kūṟiṉ, avaṟṟiṟ kiṭaiyē kāṇappaṭum vēṟṟumai mikutiyāl, avai oṉṟuṭa ṉeṉṟu vēṟupaṭukiṉṟaṉa eṉpataic cāṉṟukaḷuṭaṉ eṭuttu viḷakkik kāṭṭutalaiviṭṭu, avaiyellām orē mūla moḻiyaic cārntavaiyē eṉṟum, am mūla moḻi tirāviṭa moḻitāṉ eṉṟum cāṉṟukaḷuṭaṉ īṇṭu viḷakkippōtalē, kaṭamaiyāyiṟṟu.





tirāviṭa moḻikaḷ vaṭamoḻiyiṉ vēṟāṉavai:

taṅkaḷukkut terinta ovvoṉṟum pārppaṉarkaḷiṭamiruntu peṟappaṭṭatākattā ṉirukka vēṇṭumeṉṟu vaṭamoḻip paṇṭitarkaḷ iyalpākavē nampivantārkaḷ. ataṉai yoṭṭiyē avarkaḷ tirāviṭa moḻikaḷ, vaṭa intiya moḻi marapukaḷiliruntu ettuṇaiyō māṟupaṭṭiruppiṉum, vaṭamoḻi yiṉattiliruntu peṟappaṭṭavaikaḷē eṉṟu cātittuvantaṉar. itaṉaip paṇṭaiya airōppiya ārāycciyāḷarkaḷum eḷitil nampivantaṉar. avarkaḷ ārāynta tirāviṭa moḻikaḷ ovvoṉṟilum ōraḷaviṟku vaṭamoḻic coṟkaḷ tatpavamākavum, tatcamamākavum kalantirukkak kaṇṭaṉar. āṉāl, am moḻikaḷil vaṭamoḻik kalappillāta coṟkaḷum, marapumoḻikaḷum pala iruntaṉa veṉpataiyum, avaiyē ammoḻikaḷiṉ ciṟappiyalpukaḷ eṉpataiyum, avaṟṟilētāṉ avvam moḻikaḷiṉ taṉippaṭṭa uyirnilai amaintukiṭantatu eṉpataiyum avarkaḷ kaṇṭaṟintukoḷḷavillai. ataṉ payaṉāka, avarkaḷ ammoḻikaḷiṟ kāṇappaṭum vaṭamoḻiyaic cārntaṉavallāta pakutika ḷellām yātō oru paṇṭaiya puṟanāṭṭu moḻiyaic cārntavai eṉṟu kūṟivantaṉar. ak koḷkaippaṭi tirāvirarkaḷukkum,[1] keḷararkaḷukku[2]miṭaiyē mutaṉmaiyāṉa vēṟupāṭukaḷ kiṭaiyā. ataṟkukkāraṇam, vaṅkāḷi mutaliya keḷariya iṉattaic cērnta moḻika ḷellām vaṭamoḻic citaivukaḷēyāyiṉum, avaṟṟuḷ vaṭamoḻi yallāta coṟkaḷum, marapumoḻikaḷum oru cilavē kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa eṉpatē. avaṟṟaip pōṉṟavaiyē tirāviṭa moḻikaḷuḷ kāṇappaṭum vaṭamoḻic cārpaṟṟa coṟkaḷum, marapu moḻikaḷum eṉṟu avarkaḷ ūkittamai iyalpē. eṉavē, vaṭamoḻiyiliruntutāṉ tirāviṭa moḻikaḷ tōṉṟiṉa eṉṟu muṭivukaṭṭik kokkarittu vantārkaḷ kōlpuruk, kāri, vilkiṉs mutaliya airōppiya ārāycciyāḷarkaḷ. ivarkaḷ vaṭamoḻi yārāycciyil vallunarēyāyiṉum tirāviṭa moḻikaḷaip paṟṟiya varaiyil mikavuṅ kuṟainta aṟivuṭaiyavarkaḷeṉṟē, avaṟṟaippaṟṟi oṉṟumē teriyātavarkaḷeṉṟētāṉ koḷḷalvēṇṭum. oppilakkaṇattiṉ uṇmaikaḷait terinta evarum tirāviṭa moḻikaḷiṉ ilakkaṇa amaitikaḷaiyum, coṟṟokutikaḷaiyum, vaṭamoḻi ilakkaṇa amaitikaḷōṭum, coṟṟokutikaḷōṭum oppiṭṭup pārttapiṉ, avai ekkāraṇattaik koṇṭum oṉṟaṟ koṉṟu uṟavuṭaiyaṉa veṉṟō, vaṭamoḻiyiliruntutāṉ tirāviṭa moḻi ilakkaṇa amaippum, collākkat tiripukaḷum tōṉṟiyiruttal vēṇṭum eṉṟō kūṟa muṉvaramāṭṭār eṉpatu tiṇṇam.

tirāviṭa moḻikaḷukkum vaṭamoḻikku maṭṭumēyaṉṟi, av viraṇṭiṟkum intu-airōppiya[3] moḻikaḷukkumē ētō oruvakaiyāṉa mūlat toṭarpu iruntirukkavēṇṭumeṉṟu koḷvatō, ak koḷkaiyē aṭippaṭaiyāka, intu-airōppiya moḻiyiṉattil tirā viṭa moḻikaḷukkum iṭampeṟa urimai yuṇṭu eṉṟu koḷvatō, vaṭamoḻiyi liruntē tirāviṭa moḻikaḷ tōṉṟi yiruttal vēṇṭum eṉṟa koḷkaikku muṟṟilum vēṟupaṭṭa koḷkaikaḷākum. ilakkaṇa amaippu muṟaikaḷaiyum coṟ ṟokutikaḷaiyum ārāyntāl, mēṟ kuṟitta intu - airōppiya moḻiyiṉattil tirāviṭa moḻikaḷukkum iṭamuṇṭu eṉṟu niṟuvutal oruvāṟu iyalum. āriyar varukaikku muṟpaṭṭa kālattilēyē cittiya[4] moḻiyiṉattiliruntu intu-airōppiya moḻiyiṉaṅkaḷ pirintuviṭṭaṉa. im muṭipu piṉṉiṣ[5] moḻiyilum, turukkiya moḻiyilum kāṇappaṭum cila paṇṭai mūlac coṟkaḷāl valiyuṟukiṟatu; atupōlavē, tirāviṭa moḻikaḷilum āriyar varukaikku muṟpaṭṭa kālattu vaḻaṅkiyiruntaṉavākak karutakkūṭiya cila moḻic coṟkaḷ payiṉṟu varukiṉṟamai mēṟkūṟiya muṭipai valiyuṟuttuvatākum. ataṉāl, tirāviṭa moḻikaḷ ic coṟkaḷai vaṭamoḻiyiliruntu peṟṟaṉa eṉṟē, aṉṟi iraṇṭu moḻikaḷukkum potuvāṉa oru paṇṭaip pākata moḻiyiliruntu peṟṟirukkakkūṭum eṉṟē koḷvatu tavaṟeṉpatum, arukil vaḻaṅkiya tirāviṭa moḻikaḷi liruntu vaṭamoḻi pala coṟkaḷaip peṟṟup payaṉpaṭuttap piṉvāṅkavillai yeṉṟu koḷvatē cariyāṉa muṭipeṉpatum, eḷitil ūkikkappaṭum.

eḵtu eppaṭiyāyiṉum, vaṭamoḻiyiliruntu tirāviṭa moḻikaḷ piṟantirukkavēṇṭum eṉṟu koṇṭa paḻaṅ koḷkai kuruṭṭuk koḷkaiyē eṉpatil aiyamēyillai. inti, vaṅkāḷi mutaliya keḷariya moḻikaḷ vaṭamoḻiyiliruntu tōṉṟiyirukkakkūṭu mātalāl, tirāviṭa moḻikaḷum avvāṟētāṉ tōṉṟiyirukka vēṇṭum eṉṟu koḷḷutal cālpāmō? ākātu. kīḻvarum uṇmaikaḷaic carivā uyttuṇarāmaiyē tavaṟāṉa im muṭipukkuk kāraṇamākum.

(1) tirāviṭa moḻikaḷil vaṭamoḻic cārpillāta taṉip pakuti, vaṭamoḻic cārpuḷḷa pakutiyiṉum, mikamika virintatoṉṟu eṉpataip paṇṭaiyārāycciyāḷarkaḷ puṟakkaṇittuviṭṭaṉar.

(2) oru moḻiyiṉ uyirnilaikaḷ eṉṟu karutap paṭuvaṉavāṉa iṭappeyarkaḷ, viṉaittiripukaḷ, peyart tiripukaḷ, collākka muṟai mutaliya ellāvaṟṟilum tirāviṭa moḻikaḷ vaṭamoḻiyiṉiṉṟu aṭiyōṭu māṟupaṭukiṉṟaṉa eṉṟa kaṇkūṭāṉa uṇmaiyaiyum avarkaḷ puṟakkaṇittuviṭṭārkaḷ.

(3) tirāviṭa moḻikaḷum vaṭamoḻiyiṉiṉṟu tōṉṟiṉavē eṉṟu koṇṭa mēṉāṭṭārāycciyāḷar taṅ koḷkaikkut tirāviṭa moḻikaḷil akkālai veḷiyiṭappaṭṭirunta akara varicaikaḷaiyē[6] ātāramākak koṇṭiruntaṉar. avaṟṟuḷ vaṭamoḻic coṟkaḷiṟ perumpālaṉa māṟutaliṉṟi appaṭiyē kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa ; māṟutal peṟṟa cila coṟkaḷ tam vaṭamoḻit toṭarpai naṉku terivippaṉavākak kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. āṉāl, tirāviṭa moḻip pulavarkaḷ ic coṟkaḷait tirāviṭac coṟkaḷākak koḷḷāmal, (vaṭamoḻiyiliruntu vanta) ticaic coṟkaḷākavē koṇṭaṉar eṉpatum, avaṟṟiṉ tiripaḷaviṟkēṟṟavāṟu avai avarkaḷāl oḻuṅkupaṭuttip patippikkappaṭṭaṉa eṉpatum pāvam! av vārāycciyāḷarkkut teriyātu. aṉṟiyum, vaṭamoḻic cārpaṟṟa coṟkaḷiṉ tokutiyē perum pakutiyākum eṉpatai uṇarntu, ac coṟkaḷait taṉiyākap pirittu muṟaippaṭutti nāṭṭu moḻic coṟkaḷ allatu “ceñcoṟkaḷ” eṉṟu pārāṭṭivantaṉar eṉpatai av vārāycciyāḷar yāṅkaṉam aṟintiruppar?

uṇmaiyil tirāviṭa moḻikaḷiṟ kāṇappaṭum vaṭa coṟkaḷaiyum, citaivukaḷaiyum pirittaṟital aritaṉṟu. orucila coṟkaḷē vaṭa collā, tirāviṭac collā eṉṟu ārāyntaṟitaṟ kuriyaṉavām. nīr eṉpatum mīṉ eṉpatum iru moḻiyāḷarālum tattam moḻiccoṟkaḷ eṉṟu urimai pārāṭṭappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. eṉiṉum, iraṇṭum tirāviṭac coṟkaḷ eṉpatē ēṟpuṭaittākum.

(ovvoru moḻiyilum neruṅkiya uṟaviṉarkaḷaiyum, uṭaluṟuppukkaḷaiyum, eṉṟum māṟātaṉavum potuppaṭaiyāṉavaiyumāṉa iravu, pakal, ñāyiṟu, maram, kal, vīṭu, yāṉai, kutirai pōṉṟa poruḷkaḷaiyuṅ kuṟikkuñ coṟkaḷ avvam moḻiyiṉ mutaṉmaiyāṉa coṟkaḷākak karutappaṭum. iṭap peyarkaḷum eṇṇup peyarkaḷum māṟupaṭutal iyalpākalāṉ avaṟṟai mutaṉmaic coṟkaḷōṭu īṇṭuc cērppataṟkillai. piṉvarum paṭṭiyil, oppunōkki uyattaṟitaṟ keḷitāka vaṭa moḻiyiliruntum, tamiḻiliruntum 60 mutaṉmaic coṟkaḷ tarappaṭukiṉṟaṉa.) ﻿(iṭappeyarkaḷ eṇṇuppeyarkaḷ nīṅkalāka) vaṭamoḻi, tamiḻmoḻikaḷiliruntu eṭukkappaṭṭa 60 mutaṉmaic coṟkaḷ aṭaṅkiya paṭṭi.

aḻu rut‿

kol इन haṉ

(4) mēlum, vaṭamoḻiyi liruntu tōṉṟiyaṉavē tirāviṭa moḻikaḷ eṉṟu nampiya mēṉāṭṭārāyc ciyāḷarkaḷukku, vaṭa coṟkaḷaiyē eṭuttāḷāmalō, aṅkoṉṟum iṅkoṉṟumāka mikavum arukiya nilaiyilmaṭṭum eṭuttāṇṭō varum tiruntā moḻikaḷum tirāviṭa moḻi yiṉattil irukkiṉṟaṉa eṉpatē teriyātu. aṉṟiyum, vaṭacoṟkaḷai ōraḷaviṟku eṭuttāḷum tiruntiya tirāviṭa moḻikaḷum kaṭṭāyamāka avaṟṟai eṭuttāḷattāṉ vēṇṭumeṉpatillāmal, viruppamuḷatēṟ payaṉpaṭuttiyum, iṉṟēl aṟavē yotukkiyum varukiṉṟaṉa eṉpatum avarkaḷukkut teriyātu. āṉāl, iṉṟaiya nilaiyai nōkkiṉ, vaṭamoḻiyai aṟavē nīkkuva teṉpatu teluṅku moḻikku aritākum; kaṉṉaṭa moḻikkō mikavum aritu; malaiyāḷa moḻikkō aritiṉumaritu. im moḻikaḷ kaṇakku vaḻakkillāmal vaṭa coṟkaḷai eṭuttāṇṭuvantuḷḷamaiyālum, ac coṟkaḷiṉ utaviyai nāṭuvatē vaḻakkamākak koṇṭuḷḷamaiyālum, tattam ciṟappup paṇpukaḷai yiḻantu, taṉittu niṉṟiyaṅkum āṟṟalaiyum iḻantu niṟkiṉṟaṉa. āṉāl, tirāviṭa moḻikaḷ aṉaittiṉum mikavum tiruntiya paṇpaṭṭa nilaiyiluḷḷa tamiḻmoḻiyō, vēṇṭumeṉṟāl, vaṭacoṟkaḷai aṟavē akaṟṟit taṉittiyaṅkum āṟṟal vāyntiruppatōṭaṉṟi, avaṟṟiṉ utavi yillāmal mikavum mēmpaṭṭu, vaḷamuṟṟu miḷirum āṟṟalum vāyntatākum. paṇṭait tūya tamiḻmoḻikkuc centamiḻ eṉṟu peyar. ic centamiḻilēyē tamiḻmoḻiyiṟ kāṇappaṭum paṇṭai ilakkiyaṅkaḷiṟ perumpālaṉa iyaṉṟuḷḷaṉa. āṉāl, avaṟṟuḷ kāṇappaṭum vaṭacoṟkaḷō mikamikak kuṟaivu. iṉṟaiya pēccut tamiḻuṭa ṉum, urainaṭait tamiḻuṭaṉum innilai māṟupaṭṭatoṉṟākak kāṇappaṭum. etaṉāl avvāṟeṉilō, kaṇṇuṅ karuttumāy iruntu vaṭacoṟkaḷaiyum, vaṭamoḻi yeḻuttukkaḷaiyum vilakki, tūya tamiḻccoṟkaḷ, marapumoḻikaḷ, amaippukaḷ ākiyavaṟṟaiyē kaiyāṇṭu vantuḷḷamaiyiṉālē tāṉ. tamiḻmakkaḷiṭaiyē ic centamiḻ moḻip pātukāppu evvaḷavukkup paraviyirunta teṉpatu, eḻutappaṭṭa oru tamiḻ nūlil evvaḷavuk kevvaḷavu vaṭa coṟkaḷ arukik kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉavō, avvaḷavukku avvaḷavu atu ciṟantatoru nūleṉṟum, evvaḷavuk kevvaḷavu vaṭacoṟkaḷ mikutiyākak kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉavō avvaḷavukkavvaḷavu atu tāḻntatoru nūleṉṟum karuti matippiṭum paḻakkam paṇṭutoṭṭup payiṉṟu varukiṉṟamaiyiṉālēyē iṉitu teḷiyappaṭum. piṟa moḻi nūlkaḷ cilavaṟṟil evvaḷavuk kevvaḷavu vaṭa moḻic coṟkaḷ payiṉṟu varukiṉṟaṉavō avvaḷavuk kavvaḷavu an nūlkaḷ avvammoḻiyiṉarāṟ ciṟappuṭaṉ pōṟṟappaṭum; tamiḻilō evvaḷavukkevvaḷavu tamiḻ nūlkaḷ vaṭamoḻiyiṉ utaviyai nāṭāmal taṉittiyaṅku kiṉṟaṉavō avvaḷavuk kavvaḷavu ciṟappuṭaṉ pōṟṟappaṭum. uṇṇāṭṭuc ciṟṟūrkaḷilum, nāṭṭuppuṟaṅkaḷilum vāḻntuvarum tāḻnta makkaḷiṭaiyē vaṭamoḻic coṟkaḷaip pēccuvaḻakkilum kaiyāḷāmal otukkum tūya paḻakkam kāṇappaṭukiṉṟatu. oru moḻiyiṉ toṉmaic ciṟappunilai am moḻiyiliyalum ceyyuḷkaḷilum, tāḻnta kuṭimakkaḷiṉ pēccukaḷilumiruntē ārāyntu kāṇappaṭum eṉpatu oru potu uṇmaiyākum. piṟ kālattil mikavum valintu muyaṉṟu eḻutappaṭṭa tamiḻ urainaṭai nūlkaḷilum, pārppaṉarkaḷ pēcum tamiḻilum, mikavum kaṟṟavarkaḷākak karutappaṭum tamiḻarkaḷiṉ pēccilumē vaṭamoḻi mikaipaṭap pāvi vaḻaṅkukiṉṟatu. atuvum, camaya uṇmai, aṟiviyal, tattuvam, eṉak kalaikaḷiluḷḷa marapuc coṟkaḷ ākiyavaṟṟai viḷakkumiṭaṅ kaḷilmaṭṭumē ivvāṟu vaṭacoṟkaḷ perumpāṉmaiyum kaiyāḷappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. āṉāl, ivvāṟu kāṇappaṭum vaṭa coṟkaḷiṉ tokai, āṅkila nūlkaḷiṟ kaiyāḷappaṭṭuk kāṇappaṭum ilattiṉ moḻic coṟkaḷiṉ tokaiyai viṭa mikutiyāṉatoṉṟaṉṟu.

eṭuttukkāṭṭāka, āṅkila viviliya nūlilum, tamiḻ viviliya nūlilum ”pattuk kaṭṭaḷaikaḷ” eṉṟa pakutiyai oppumaikkāka eṭuttukkoṇṭu avaṟṟiṉ col tokutikaḷai ārāyvōm. āṅkilac coṟkaḷai ārāyu miṭattu nēriṭaiyāṉa ilattīṉ coṟkaḷaiyum, nār maṉ pireñcu mūlamāka vanta ilattīṉ citaivukaḷaiyum oṉṟākac cērttu ilattīṉākak kaṇakkiṭuvōm. ataṉ palaṉ kīḻvarum paṭṭikaippaṭi yākum.

col vakai ﻿āṅkilam ﻿tamiḻ

mottam ilattīṉ tūya āṅkilam mottam vaṭamoḻi tamiḻ

peyarum peyaruriyum 43 14 29 53 21 32

viṉai 20 7 13 34 7 27

eṇṇup peyar 5 0 5 6 1 5

”āyiram” eṉpatu vaṭamoḻip peyar. ataṉālēyē eṇṇup peyari ṉetiril vaṭamoḻiyiṉ kīḻ ’1’ eṉṟu kāṇappaṭukiṟatu. mottamākak kaṇakkiṭṭāl āṅkilattil varum ilattīṉum, tamiḻil varum vaṭamoḻiyum orē paṭittāka nūṟṟukku 45 viḻukkāṭu ākiṟatu. iraṇṭilum iṭap peyarkaḷ, uriccoṟkaḷ, urupukaḷ, iṭaiccoṟkaḷ, ilakkaṇa uṟuppukkaḷ ivai muṟṟilum tāymoḻiyaic cērntavaiyēyākum.

āṅkilamoḻi taṉ vaḷarccimuṟaiyil tūya āṅkilō cāksaṉ moḻiyi liruntum, ilattīṉ moḻiyiliruntum evvakaiyil utavipeṟṟirukkiṟatu eṉpatai āṅkila camayat talaivar ṭreñc[1] piṉvarumāṟu viḷakkik kūṟiyuḷḷār:-

“im moḻiyi (āṅkilam)ṉuṭaiya iṇaippukaḷ, oli muṟai, nāṭi narampukaḷ, tacai nārkaḷ, iṭaccoṟkaḷ, urupukaḷ, iṭaiccoṟkaḷ, eṇṇup peyarkaḷ, tuṇai viṉaikaḷ, yāppu muṟaikkup payaṉpaṭum ciṟuciṟu acaic coṟkaḷ, ilakkaṇa muṟaiyamaippu ākiyavai ellām taṉippaṭṭa āṅkilō cāksaṉē. it teyvikak kaṭṭiṭattiṟku ilattīṉ moḻi utaviyavai ceṅkaṟkaḷum, naṉku cetukkik kaṭaica liṭappaṭṭa calavaik kaṟkaḷumē yām; āṉāl ivai yellāvaṟṟaiyum patittup piṇaittuk kaṭṭiṭamāka eḻuppuvikka utaviya nīṟu, cuṇṇāmpu, cāntu ellām tūya āṅkilō cāksaṉē’’

ikkūṟṟu appaṭiyē tirāviṭattiṟkum vaṭamoḻikkum poruntuvatākum; mēṟ pakutiyil “āṅkilō cāksaṉ” eṉṟu varumiṭaṅkaḷi lellām, “tirāviṭam” eṉpataiyum, ”ilattīṉ” eṉṟu varumiṭaṅkaḷilellām ”vaṭamoḻi” eṉpataiyum amaittukkoṇṭu paṭittāl tirāviṭa moḻikkum vaṭamoḻikkum iṭaiyē et tuṇai yuṟavu irukkiṟa teṉpatu teḷḷat teḷiya viḷaṅkum.

mēṟ “pattuk kaṭṭaḷaikaḷ” eṉṟa tamiḻ viviliya nūṟpakutiyil kāṇappaṭum vaṭacoṟṟokaiyum āṅkila nūṟpakutiyil kāṇappaṭum ilattīṉ coṟṟokaiyum viyakkattakka vaṇṇam oṉṟākak kāṇappaṭukiṉṟateṉ patai aṟintōm. ataṉāl āṅkilam ilattīṉukku evvaḷaviṟkuk kaṭaṉpaṭṭuḷḷatō, avvaḷaviṟkut tamiḻ vaṭamoḻikkuk kaṭaṉpaṭṭirukkavēṇṭum eṉṟu colvatu tavaṟākum. āṅkilap pakuti taṉmāṭṭuḷḷa ilattīṉ coṟkaḷai nīkkiviṭa muyaṉṟāl, ataṉ poruḷ nuṭpaṅ keṭṭuviṭum. āṉāl, tamiḻp pakuti taṉmāṭṭuḷḷa vaṭa coṟkaḷil perumpakutiyaiyō, aṉṟi muḻuvataiyumō nīkkiviṭṭut takka tamiḻc coṟkaḷaip peytukoṇṭāl evvakaiyiluṅ kuṟaivuṟātu; avvāṟu nīkkuvatāl ataṉ tūymai māṟṟum, cemmaip paṇpum uyarntoḷirumē yaṉṟik kuṟaintu maṅkā. ilattīṉiliruntu peṟṟa coṟkaḷiṟ palavaṟṟiṟku āṅkilō cāksaṉil nērc coṟkaḷ kiṭaiyā; eṉavē, ac coṟkaḷai nīkkiviṭuva teṉṟāl avaṟṟiṉ karuttukkaḷai nīṇṭa, aḻakaṟṟa coṟṟoṭar kaḷāltāṉ viḷakkavēṇṭum. āṉāl tamiḻō, taṉippaṭṭa vakaiyil colvaḷam niṟaintatu. appaṭiyirukka aḵtēṉ vaṭacoṟkaḷai eṭuttāṇṭa teṉṟāl, colvaḷa miṉmaiyāṉaṉṟu; putumai karutiyēyām eṉṟu viṭukka. ”pattuk kaṭṭaḷaikaḷ” eṉṟa viviliya nūṟpakutiyai nāṭṭuppuṟat tāḻnta makkaḷiṉ pēccu muṟaiyil amaittāl, atiluḷḷa vaṭacoṟkaḷiṉ tokai mikavuṅ kuṟaintu viṭum. ataṉaiyē cemmaippaṭuttit tirutti ilakkiya naṭaiyil amaippōmāṉāl, atil eñciyuḷḷa oṉ ṟiraṇṭu vaṭacoṟkaḷum māyamāyp pōyviṭum. appakutiyiṟ kāṇappaṭum vaṭa coṟkaḷuḷ oṉṟē oṉṟiṟkuttāṉ kuṟṟañ collāta vakaiyil nērt tamiḻccol amaikka muṭiyātu. ac col ”vikkirakam”[2] eṉpatu ac collum ataṉ karuttum tamiḻmakkaḷiṉ paḻakka vaḻakkaṅkaḷukkum, koḷkaikaḷukkum puṟampāṉavai. pārppaṉarkaḷāl purāṇak karuttukaḷōṭum, ”vikkiraka ārātaṉai muṟai” eṉṉum uruva vaḻipāṭṭōṭum, ic collum karuttum tamiḻnāṭṭiṉuṭ pukuttap paṭṭaṉavākum. pārppaṉarkaḷiṉ camayattiṟku in nāṭṭil mutalil irunta celvākkiṉālēyē, camayak karuttukkaḷait terivippaṉavāyt tamiḻ nūṟkaḷiṟ kāṇappaṭum coṟkaḷiṟ perumpālaṉa vaṭacoṟkaḷākavō, ac coṟ citaivukaḷākavō kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. ēṟṟa tirāviṭac coṟkaḷ illai eṉpatillai; muṟṟilum ēṟṟavaiyum, orōvaḻi vaṭacoṟkaḷiṉum ciṟantaṉavākavuṅ koḷḷak kūṭiya coṟkaḷ uḷa. avai yellām, iṉṟu vaḻak kiḻantu, maṟaintu, ceyyuḷ vaḻakkilmaṭṭum aruki vaḻaṅkukiṉṟaṉa. tamiḻk kalvi maṅkiyuḷḷa innāḷil, urai naṭaiyil ac coṟkaḷ vaḻaṅkumēl an naṭai mikavum karaṭumuraṭāṉa teṉṟum, kaṭapaṭām pōṉṟa teṉṟum karuti ikaḻappaṭum. tamiḻc camaya nūlkaḷil vaṭacoṟkaḷ potuvāka eṭuttāḷappaṭ ṭuḷḷamaikku ituvē, uṇmaiyāṉa kāraṇamum, orē kāraṇamumākum.

ēṉaiya tirāviṭa moḻikaḷil epporuḷaik kuṟitteḻunta kaṭṭuraiyāyiṉuñ cari, ceyyuḷāyiṉuñ cari, ataṉkaṇ mikutiyāṉa vaṭacoṟkaḷ payiṉṟirukkak kāṇalām. avai putumai karuti eṭuttāḷappaṭṭaṉavalla; kiṭṭattaṭṭa iṉṟiyamaiyātaṉavākavē karutik kaiyāḷap paṭṭuḷḷaṉa. inta nilaimai vantataṟkuk kāraṇam am moḻikaḷiṉ ilakkiya vaḷarcci mutaṉmaiyākap pārppaṉarkaḷ kaiyilēyē oppaṭaikkappaṭṭu vantuḷḷamaiyēyām. teluṅku moḻiyai yeṭuttukkoṇṭāl, am moḻiyil ilakkaṇa nūlkaḷ eḻutiyavarkaḷuḷḷum, ilakkiyaṅkaḷ eḻutiyavarkaḷuḷḷum mutaṉmaiyāṉavarkaḷum, peyar pōṉavarkaḷum pārppaṉarkaḷē. am moḻiyiṟ kāṇappaṭum nūlkaḷuḷ oṉṟē oṉṟutāṉ pārppaṉa rallātārāṉa āciriyaroruvarāl iyaṟṟappaṭṭatākum. uṇmaiyākap pārttāl, teluṅkumakkaḷ eṉṟu aḻaippataṟku evvakaiyilum uriyārāṉa teluṅkup pārppaṉa rallāta tokutiyiṉar taṅkaḷ moḻivaḷarcci, kalaivaḷarcci ākiya vaṟṟaip pārppaṉarkaḷiṭamē oppaṭaittuviṭṭavarkaḷākak kāṇappaṭukiṉṟārkaḷ.

tamiḻilō ippaṭi yillai. nūl eṉṟa peyarukkuriya ṉavāyp pōṟṟic cēmittuvaikkavēṇṭiya vakaiyil pārppaṉarkaḷāl eḻutappaṭṭaṉavāyk kāṇakkūṭiyavai mikamikac cilavē. tamiḻmakkaḷāl avarkaḷuṭaiya tāy moḻiyākiya tamiḻiṉ vaḷarcciyum, kalaiyum, nākarikamum paṇṭutoṭṭuk kaṇṇuṅ karuttumāy ārvattuṭaṉum veṟṟiyuṭaṉum pōṟṟappaṭṭuvarukiṉṟaṉa. tamiḻ ilakkiya āciriya varicaiyil pārppaṉarkaḷ eytiyatellām uraiyāciriyar eṉṟa nilaikku mēṟpaṭṭatillai. tāḻnta vakuppiṉarāya tiruvaḷḷuvārāl eḻutappaṭṭa tirukkuṟaḷukkup pārppaṉārāṉa parimēlaḻakar eṉpār vakuttuḷḷa uraiyē mikavuñ ciṟantatoṉṟākap pārāṭṭappaṭukiṟatu.

teṉpakutiyil pēcappaṭum moḻikaḷ vaṭamoḻiyaip piṉpaṟṟiyē amaikkappaṭṭapōtilum, taṉip paṭṭa ilakkiya vaḷattai ēṟpaṭuttikkoḷḷa avaṟṟuṭ cila virumpi muyaṉṟaṉa eṉṟum, tamiḻ, teluṅku, kaṉṉa ṭam, malaiyāḷam ākiya moḻikaḷiṟ kāṇappaṭum mutaṉmai yāṉa ilakkiyaṅkaḷellām vaṭamoḻi nūlkaḷiṉ moḻi peyarppō, aṉṟi virivuraiyō ātalvēṇṭum eṉṟum, av virivurai yeḻutuvatilum vaṭamoḻit toṭarkaḷ eṭuttāḷappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa eṉṟum pērāciriyar uvilsaṉ[3] eṉpavar kūṟukiṟār. tamiḻaippaṟṟiya varaiyil ik kūṟṟu oppattakkataṉṟu. tamiḻil iraṇṭu oppaṟṟa ciṟanta nūlkaḷāka ulakattōrāl karutappaṭuvaṉa kuṟaḷum, cintāmaṇiyumām. iv viraṇṭum muṟṟilum vaṭamoḻit toṭarpaṟṟavai. tamiḻāciriyarkaḷ vaṭamoḻi nūlamaip paiyum naṭaiyaiyum piṉpaṟṟiyirukkakkūṭum; toṭarukkut toṭar moḻipeyarttuḷḷaṉar eṉpatai oppuk koḷḷa muṭiyātu. tamiḻ irāmāyaṇam, māpāratam pōṉṟa kāviyaṅkaḷ ivvāṟu eḻuntaṉavēyām. tamiḻar taṅkaḷ kamparāmāyaṇam mutaṉūlākiya vālmīki irāmāyaṇattiṉum mikavuñ ciṟantatu eṉṟu urimai pārāṭṭuvatē itaṟkuc cāṉṟākum.

(5) moḻikaḷuḷ oṉṟaṟkoṉṟaṉiṭaiyē kāṇappaṭum oṟṟumai vēṟṟumaikaḷai viḷakkap peritum payaṉpaṭum muṟaikaḷuḷ mikavum muṭivāṉatu am moḻikaḷiṉ ilakkaṇa amaippai oppiṭṭuc cīrtūkkip pārppatē yām. am muṟaippaṭi tirāviṭa moḻikaḷ vaṭamoḻi yiṉum vēṟāṉavai yeṉṟum, tirāviṭa moḻikaḷai intu-airōppiya moḻiyiṉattōṭu iṇaippataiviṭa, cittiya moḻiyiṉattōṭu iṇaippatē cālpuṭaittākum eṉṟum tuṇintu kūṟalām.



↑ Archbishop Trench

↑ Image

↑ Professor Wilson





﻿5. ilakkaṇa amaippil vaṭamoḻikkum tirāviṭa moḻikaḷukkum iṭaiyēyuḷḷa vēṟupāṭukaḷ



(i) tirāviṭa moḻikaḷil uyiraṟṟa poruḷkaḷ maṭṭu maṉṟi, pakuttaṟivaṟṟa uyirkaḷum potuppāl (aḵṟiṇai) ākavē matikkappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. āṇpāl, peṇpāl vakuppu, iṭap peyarkaḷuḷ, atilum paṭarkkaiyilmaṭṭumē, kāṇappaṭukiṟatu. peyaruriccoṟkaḷilum, paṭarkkai viṉaikaḷilum ip paṭarkkaip peyariṉ tiripē vikutiyāy niṉṟu pāl uṇarttukiṉṟatu. uyirvakaikaḷiṉ peyarkaḷ pālvakai kāṭṭavēṇṭiṉ āṇ peṇ eṉṟa peyarkaḷaic cērttē yākavēṇṭum; appōtum avai āṇ allatu peṇ pālāvatillai; potuppālēyākum. ēṉeṉil, avaṟṟaik kuṟikkum iṭappeyarkaḷum, avaṟṟiṉ payaṉilaiyāṉa viṉaikaḷum potuppāl (aḵṟiṇai) muṭivaiyē koḷḷalvēṇṭum. ittakaiya pālvakuppu vaṭamoḻi mutaliya intu - airōppiya moḻikaḷiṉ pakaṭṭāṉa puṉaiviyal pālpākupāṭṭiṉiṉṟum muṟṟilum māṟupaṭṭatu. āṉāl cittiyak kuḻuviṉ vaḻakkukku iḵtu ottatu.

(ii) tirāviṭa moḻikaḷil peyarc coṟkaḷ vēṟṟumai ēṟkumpōtu vikutiyēṟṟu urumāṟuva tillai; ac coṟkaḷiṉiṉṟu eḷitil pirikkakkūṭiya piṉ aṭaikaḷ allatu urupukaḷaiyē ēṟṟu māṟukiṉṟaṉa. mēlum paṉmaip peyariṉ vēṟṟumai yuruvāṉatu urupukaḷai ēṟku muṉ paṉmai vikuti peṟuvatoṉṟait tavira maṟṟev vakaiyilum orumai vēṟṟumai yuruviliruntu māṟupaṭuvatillai; cittiya moḻikaḷum ivvāṟē.[1]

(iii) tirāviṭa moḻikaḷiṉ aḵṟiṇaip peyarkaḷukkup paṉmai perumpālum vaḻaṅkuvatillai; viṉaic collil iḵtu iṉṉum arumai.

(iv) tirāviṭa moḻiyiṉ nāṉkām vēṟṟumai yurupākiya ku-ki-allatu ke vaṭamoḻi allatu intu - airōppiya (āriya) moḻikaḷiluḷḷa enta vēṟṟumai vikutiyuṭaṉum ottatāka illai. āṉāl atē camayattil atu kīḻait turukkiyuṭaṉum[2], pehistaṉ[3] paṭṭayaṅkaḷuṭaṉum piṉṉiṣ moḻiyiṉaṅkaḷuṭaṉum toṭar puṭaiyatāyirukkiṉṟatu.

(v) intu-airōppiya moḻikaḷil muṉ ṉurupukaḷ[4] allatu oṭṭukkaḷ (pirattiyayam) payaṉpaṭuttappaṭum iṭaṅkaḷil ellām tirāviṭa moḻikaḷum, cittiyak kuḻu moḻikaḷum piṉṉurupukaḷai allatu oṭṭukkaḷaip payaṉpaṭuttukiṉṟaṉa. iv urupukaḷum, oṭṭukkaḷum ilakkaṇa muṟaippaṭi taṉiccolvakaikaḷ alla, peyarccol tiripukaḷēyām. ivvāṟē avaṟṟiṉ viṉai uriccoṟkaḷum peyar allatu toḻiṟ peyar allatu viṉai yeccaṅkaḷē yākum. avai eppōtum tām taḻuvum viṉaikaḷukku muntiyē varukiṉṟaṉa.

(vi) vaṭamoḻiyilum, intu-airōppiya moḻikaḷilum peyaruric coṟkaḷ peyarc coṟkaḷaip pōlavē pāl, eṇ, iṭam ēṟkiṉṟaṉa. tirāviṭa moḻikaḷilum, cittiya moḻikaḷilum avai pāl mutaliyavai ēṟpa tillai. ip peyaruric coṟkaḷ uṇmaiyil paṇpup peyarkaḷi liruntu vantavaiyē yākum. paṇpup peyar eṉṟu taṉippaṭa niṟkumpōtu, avai piṟa peyarkaḷ pōlavē tiṇai pāl mutaliyavai ēṟpiṉum, piṟa peyarka ḷuṭaṉ oṭṭappeṟṟu uriccollākap payaṉpaṭumpōtu aṅṅaṉam eṟpatillai.

(vii) iṉṉeru vakaiyil tirāviṭa moḻikaḷ maṅkōliyam, mañcu mutalāṉa cittiya moḻikaḷuṭaṉ ottum intu - airōppiya moḻiyiṉattuṭaṉ māṟupaṭṭum uḷḷaṉa. aḵtāvatu, iyalumpō tellām im moḻikaḷ peyaruric collai vaḻaṅkāmal, viṉaiccolliṉ peyarecca urupaiyē vaḻaṅkukiṉṟaṉa. (uyar maram - eṉpataṟku uyarnta maram eṉpatupōla eṉka.) ivvaḻakkattiṉ payaṉāyp paṇpup peyarkaḷaip peyaruḷiyāka vaḻaṅkumpōtu kūṭa, ataṉuṭaṉ peyaruriccol vikutiyāka ’āṉa’ eṉṟa peyareccam cērttukkoḷḷappaṭu kiṟatu. [civappāṉa.]

(viii) taṉmaip paṉmaiyil muṉṉilaiyai uṭpaṭuttiyum uṭpaṭuttāmalum iraṇṭu vakaikaḷ tirāviṭa moḻikkum,cittiya moḻikaḷukkumē ciṟappāka irukkiṉṟaṉa. vaṭa moḻiyilum, intu - airōppiya moḻiyilum iṅṅaṉam illai. itaṉai yoppatāy oruvāṟu kuṟikkakkūṭiyatu piṟkuṟitta moḻikaḷiluḷḷa irumai[5] eṉṟa eṇṇēyākum.

(ix) tirāviṭa moḻikaḷil ceyappāṭṭu viṉaikkut taṉi vaṭivam kiṭaiyātu. paṭu eṉṉum tuṇaiviṉai cērkkappaṭṭē ap poruḷil vaḻaṅkappeṟukiṟatu. (x) tirāviṭa moḻikaḷum, cittiya moḻikaḷum intu-airōppiya moḻikaḷaip pōla iṇaippu iṭaiccoṟkaḷaip[6] payaṉpaṭuttāmal "ceytu" eṉṟa viṉaiyeccattaiyē vaḻaṅkukiṉṟaṉa.

(xi) uṭaṉpāṭu, etirmaṟai eṉṟa iv viāṇṭu viṉaivaṭivaṅka ḷiruppatu tirāviṭa moḻikaṭkum, vaṭamoḻikkum iṭaiyē kāṇappaṭum talaiyāya vēṟṟumaikaḷuḷ oṉṟu. cittiya moḻikaḷuṭaṉ tirāviṭa moḻikaḷukku uḷḷa oppumaikaḷil itu talaimaiyāṉatākak kāṇkiṟatu.

(xii) tirāviṭa moḻikaḷ, maṅkōliyam mañcu, mutaliyavai, ōraḷavukkup piṟa cittiya moḻikaḷ ākiya ivaṟṟiṉ taṉippaṭṭa ciṟappup paṇpukaḷuḷ oṉṟu iṇaippu iṭappeyarkaḷiṉiṭamākap peyareccaṅkaḷ[7] vaḻaṅkuvatām. ip peyareccam mukkālaṅkaḷilum uḷḷa viṉaiyeccat tuṭaṉ āṟām vēṟṟumai urupōṭotta a-eṉṉum cāriyai peṟṟu vantatu ākum. tirāviṭa moḻikaḷ etaṉilum iṇaippu iṭappeyar eṉpataṉ niḻalkūṭak kāṇappaṭavillai. kōṇṭu moḻiyil maṭṭum viṉaiyec caṅkaḷ illāmal, oḻintupōṉatai oṭṭi, hiṅki moḻiyiṉ iṇaippu iṭappeyar kaiyāḷappaṭṭu varukiṟatu.

(xiii) tirāviṭa moḻikaḷilum, cittiya moḻikaḷilum taḻuvu moḻikaḷum, taḻuvu toṭarkaḷum taḻuvappaṭum moḻikkup piṉṉatākavē varukiṉṟaṉa. intu - airōppiya moḻi iṉattil avai potuvāka muṉvarukiṉṟaṉa. eṉavē, im moḻikaḷ aṉaittilum vākkiyattiṉ mutalil eḻuvāyum, muṭivil payaṉilaiyum, eḻuvāy aṭai[8] eḻuvāykku muṉṉum, iṭaiyil ceyappaṭuporuḷum viṉai aṭaikaḷum ākavarukiṉṟaṉa; peyaraṭaikaḷum peya reccaṅkaḷum peyariṉ āṟām vēṟṟumaiyum peyarukku muṉṉum, iṭa urupukaḷum kāla urupukaḷum peyarukku muṉ aṉṟik tām taḻuvum peyarkaḷukkum avaṟṟiṉ vēṟṟumai urupukaḷukkum piṉṉākavum varukiṉṟaṉa. viṉai yeccaṅkaḷum viṉai aṭaikaḷ pōla viṉaikku muṉṉākiṉṟaṉa. ellāvaṟṟaiyum taḻuvum viṉaimuṟṟu iṟutiyilēyē niṟkiṟatu. ivvāṟu collamaippu, coṟṟoṭaramaippu muṟaiyilum cittiya moḻikaḷ tirāviṭa moḻikaḷai ottē irukkiṉṟaṉa.

mēṟkūṟiya ilakkaṇa amaitikaḷilellām tirāviṭa moḻikaḷ potuvāka intu - airōppiya moḻikaḷiṉiṉṟum vēṟupaṭṭē iruppatum, neṭunāḷ kalantu vāḻntuṅkūṭa vaṭamoḻiyiṉiṉṟum iṉṉum vēṟupaṭṭēyiruppatum kāṇka. atē camayattil, eṅkeṅkō naṭu āciyāvilum, vaṭa āciyāvilum citaṟik kiṭakkum cittiya moḻikaḷuṭaṉ avai porunti iruppatu kavaṉikkat takkatu.

tirāviṭa moḻikaḷ vēṟu, vaṭamoḻi vēṟu eṉpatu mēṟkūṟiyavaṟṟāl naṉku teriyappaṭum. tirāviṭa moḻikaḷ vaṭamoḻiyi liruntu peṟappaṭṭaṉa alla; aṉṟi ataṉuṭaṉ cērkkappaṭakkūṭiya paṇpuṭaiyaṉavu malla.

cila kuṟippiṭṭa iṭaṅkaḷil vaṭamoḻi piṟa intu-airōppiya moḻikaḷaiviṭac cittiya moḻik kuḻuvuṭaṉ uṟavuṭaiyatāyt tōṟṟuvatu oru vēḷaip paḻaṅkuṭikaḷuṭaṉ uḷḷa neṭunāṭ paḻakkattaip poṟuttatā yirukkalām. ivaṟṟuḷ oṉṟu, vaṭamoḻiyil (ṭa ṇa ṣa) eṉṟa nāaṭi aṇṇa meykaḷ iruppaṉavākum. iv vakai yil, ”moḻi yiyalil cīṉattiṉ iṭam”[9] eṉṟa nūlil eṭkiṉs eṉpār koṇṭa putiya koḷkaiyaik kuṟippiṭal vēṇṭum. urupiṉṟi vikutiyilēyē peyarkaḷiṉ vēṟṟumai amaital, col loḻuṅku, taḻuvu vākkiyaṅkaḷ talaivākkiyattiṟku muṉvaral, viṉaimuṟṟu iṟutiyil niṟṟal ākiya ilakkaṇa amaippukkaḷuḷ vaṭamoḻi āriya iṉattaiviṭat turāṉiya iṉattaiyē piṉpaṟṟi yuḷḷatu eṉa avar kūṟukiṟār. āṉāl vaṭamoḻiyai viṭa ivvakaiyil iṉṟaiya vaṭa intiya uṇṇāṭṭu moḻikaḷē vilakkiṉṟi muṟṟilum ivvoḻuṅkaip piṉ paṟṟukiṉṟaṉa. vaṭamoḻi urainaṭaiyilkūṭa oṉṟiraṇṭiṭaṅkaḷil iv voḻuṅkukku māṟupāṭu ēṟpaṭukiṟateṉpatai eṭkiṉsē eṭuttukkāṭṭukiṟār. āyiṉum potuppaṭa urainaṭaiyil iv voḻuṅkuppaṭi viṉaimuṟṟē īṟṟilum, taḻuvu vākkiyaṅkaḷ muṉṉum varukiṉṟaṉa. āṉāl ik koḷkai nām ēṟṟukkoḷḷattakka uṟutippāṭu uṭaiyataṉṟu; ēṉeṉil col oḻuṅku oṉṟaiyēpaṟṟi vakaippaṭuttuvatāyiṉ kirēkka moḻiyaiviṭa ilattīṉum, āṅkilattaiviṭa jermaṉum turāṉiyac cārpuṭaiyavai eṉṟu kūṟavēṇṭi varum.





↑ vaṭamoḻiyilum, intu-airōppiya moḻikaḷilum orumaikkum, paṉmaikkum ovvoru vēṟṟumaiyilum vevvēṟu vikutikaḷ uḷa

↑ Oriental Turkish

↑ Behistun Tablets

↑ Preposition

↑ orumai paṉmai eṉpatiṉṟi orumai, irumai, paṉmai eṉṟu eṇ mūṉṟu vaḻaṅkum

↑ ”China's Place in Philology"—Mr. Edkins





﻿﻿cu. tirāviṭamum vaṭa intiya moḻikaḷum



tirāviṭa moḻikaḷ vaṭamoḻiyi liruntu peṟappaṭṭavaiyē, avaṟṟuḷ vaṭamoḻic cārpaṟṟaṉavākak kāṇak kiṭakkum coṟkaḷ yātō oru paṇṭaip pākata moḻiyiliruntu peṟappaṭṭavai eṉṟu koṇṭirunta koḷkaikaḷ iraṇṭum piḻaipaṭṭaṉa veṉṟu kāṭṭappaṭṭatu. eṉavē, aṟiñar cilar ivaṟṟiṟku nērmāṟāṉa koḷkai yoṉṟaik kūṟa muṉvantaṉar. vaṭamoḻiyi liruntu kiḷaitta vaṭa intiya moḻikaḷuḷ kāṇappeṟum piṟa moḻik kalapput tirāviṭa moḻikaḷiṉ kūṭṭuṟavāl ēṟpaṭṭatutāṉ eṉpatu ip putuk koḷkai. ik koḷkaiyaip parappiyavarkaḷil talaiyāṉōr pampāyaic cārnta revareṇṭ ṭākṭar sṭīveṉcaṉ[1] eṉpavarum, nēpāḷattaic cārnta hāṭjsaṉ[2] eṉpavarumē. ivarkaḷ karuttu: (1) vaṭa intiya moḻikaḷ iyaṟkait tākku talkaḷālum, kāla māṟupāṭṭālum vaṭamoḻiyi liruntu kiḷaittavaiyalla; āṉāl vaṭamoḻi yallāta piṟa moḻikaḷiṉ āṟṟal mikka kalappiṉāl citaivuṟṟaṉavēyām. (2) ip piṟamoḻikaḷ tirāviṭap pēccuṭaṉ oppumai yuṭaiyaṉa; it tirāviṭap pēccē, paṇṭai niṣātarkaḷ pēccum, piṟa paḻaṅkālak kuṭikaḷiṉ pēccumākum eṉpatē.

ivviru pakutikaḷuḷ muṉṉaiyatu piṉṉēyataiviṭa uṟutippāṭuṭaiyatākum; āṉāl atuvum oruvāṟu mikaipaṭak kūṟappaṭṭateṉṟum, ataṉait tiruttiyamaikka vēṇṭum eṉṟum tōṉṟukiṉṟatu. ēṉeṉiṟ kūṟutum: mutaṉmaiyāṉa cilavaṟṟai nōkka vaṭamoḻi hintiyākavum, vaṅkāḷamākavun tiripuṟṟatu iyaṟkai māṟutalē eṉṟu niṟuvappaṭṭuḷḷatu. airōppāvil ilattīṉi liruntu eppaṭi ittāliyamum, spāṉiṣū-m kiḷait taṉavō, appaṭiyētāṉ ivaiyum. āṉāl, vaṭamoḻi ilakkaṇa amaippōṭum paṇpuc ciṟappukkaḷōṭum, vaṭa intiya moḻikaḷiṉ ilakkaṇa amaippaiyum paṇpuc ciṟappukkaḷaiyum oppiṭṭu nōkkiṉāl, avaṟṟiṭaiyē kāṇappaṭum vēṟṟumaikaḷ āriyac cārpaṟṟa tākkutalkaḷiṉ payaṉē eṉṟu oruvāṟu teḷiyalām.

atilum, palvēṟupaṭṭa vaṭa intiya moḻikaḷu ḷellām orēpaṭittāṉa ilakkaṇa vēṟṟumaikaḷ kāṇap paṭukiṉṟamai koṇṭu, itaṟkuk kāraṇam potuppaṭṭa oru tākkutalākavē irukkavēṇṭum eṉpatu eḷitil ūkikkappaṭum. ”nākarika kālattiṟku muṟpaṭṭa paḻaṅ kālac cuvaṭukaḷākiya paṇṭaiya, cemmaippaṭāta muraṭṭu moḻiccoṟkaḷē” itaṟkuk kāraṇam eṉṟu pērāciriyar uvilsaṉ kūṟukiṟār. potuvākavē, vaṭa intiya moḻikaḷuḷ ittakaiya tākkutal pattil orupaṅkum, marātti moḻiyil maṭṭum aintil orupaṅkuntāṉ eṉṟu kaṇakkiṭappaṭ ṭuḷḷamaiyāl, āriyac cārpaṟṟa kalappē kāraṇam eṉpatu naṉku teḷiyappaṭum.

vaṭamoḻi pēciya pārppaṉarkaḷ, ṣattiriyarkaḷ, vaiciyarkaḷ eṉṟa vakuppiṉarka ḷaṭaṅkiya āriyarkaḷatu varukaikku muṉṉē, vaṭa intiyāviṉ perum pakutiyil "tasyūkkaḷ, niṣātar, milēccar” eṉṟu avarkaḷāl aḻaikkappaṭṭu vanta paḻaṅkuṭi makkaḷē vāḻntuvantirun taṉar eṉpatum, ammakkaḷ cittiya iṉattaiyō, aṉṟi āriyac cārpaṟṟa vēṟōr iṉattaiyō cērntavarkaḷ eṉpatum aṉaivarālum oppukkoḷḷappaṭṭa uṇmaikaḷākum. ip paḻaṅkuṭimakkaḷ pēciya tiruntāta cittiya moḻic coṟkaḷ, paṭaiyeṭuttuvantu veṟṟipeṟṟa āriyarkaḷ tam colvaḷamum, poruṇmaiyum niṟainta vaṭamoḻi veḷḷattil āḻntupōyviṭutal iyalpē. eṉiṉum, ap paḻaṅkuṭi makkaḷiṉ moḻi orēyaṭiyāy aḻintoḻiyāmal, taṉṉai viḻuṅkavanta vaṭamoḻi yaiyē uraṅkoṇṭu tākkik kalakkit taṉṉuṭaiya coṟkaḷiṟ cilavaṟṟaip pukuttip putiyatoru matukaiyaiyum naṭaip pōkkaiyum atu mēṟkoḷḷac ceytirukkavēṇṭu meṉpatu piṉvaruṅ kāraṇaṅkaḷāl ūkikkappaṭum: (1) cittiya moḻiyiṉ ilakkaṇa amaippu nilaiyāṉatum, etirppukku īṭukoṭukkum āṟṟalum vāyntatu. (2) āriyarkaḷai nōkka ap paḻaṅkuṭikaḷ tokaiyil mikuntavarkaḷā yiruntaṉar. (3) ataṉāl, avarkaḷai āriyarāl aṟavē aḻittoḻikka muṭiyavillai. (4) mutaṟkaṇ aṭimaika ḷākkappaṭṭu nāḷāvaṭṭattil āriyak kūṭṭattiṉaruṭaṉē cērkkappaṭṭaṉar.

ik koḷkai maṟṟek koḷkaikaḷaiyum viṭa iṉṟaiya nilaimaiyait teḷivupaṭa viḷakkukiṟatu. vaṭa intiya moḻikaḷuḷ coltokutiyilmaṭṭumē vaṭa moḻic cārpu mikutiyāyiruppatum, ilakkaṇa amaippu cittiya moḻic cārpuṭaiyatā yiruppatum nōkka, am moḻikaḷ cittiyap pakutiyuṭaṉ kalanta āriya aṭippaṭai yuṭaiyavai eṉpataiviṭa, āriyattuṭaṉ kalanta cittiya aṭippaṭaiyai uṭaiya moḻikaḷ eṉṟu kūṟuvatē mikavum poruttamuṭaiyatākum. im moḻikaḷuḷ “tāttāriya allatu cālṭiya” [3] (atāvatu cittiya)k kalappu uḷḷatu eṉa mutaṉmutal kūṟiyavar car. uvilliyam jōṉs[4] āvar. itaṉai maṟuppavaruḷ talaiyāṉavar hinti moḻiyaip paṟṟi, 1872 - ēpril iṇṭiyaṉ āṇṭikkuvari[5] eṉṟa veḷiyīṭṭil kaṭṭurai oṉṟeḻutiya kireḷas pi. si. es.[6] āvar. avar hintimoḻi oṉṟai maṭṭum ārāyntuḷḷār; atilum ilakkaṇa amaippai viṭṭuc col tokuti oṉṟai maṭṭumē ārāyntuḷḷār. ākaiyāl iṉṉum ittuṟaiyil nuṇuki ārāyntu muṭipu kāṇum varaiyil, cittiya aṭippaṭai muṭipē nilavi varum.

iṉi, ṭākṭar sṭīveṉsaṉ koḷkaiyiṉ piṉṉaiya pakutiyai nōkkuvōm. vaṭa intiya moḻikaḷil vaṭa moḻi yallāta piṟa moḻik kalappu irukkiṟateṉpatu oppukkoḷḷappaṭiṉ, ap piṟamoḻi tirāviṭa moḻi yiṉattaic cērntatē eṉpatu ap pakutiyākum. ik koḷkai avvaḷavu tiṭṭamāka ārāyntu niṟuvappaṭuva toṉṟaṉṟu. ik koḷkaippaṭi (1) intiyāviluḷḷa moḻikaḷ (vaṭa intiya moḻikaḷāyiṉuñ cari, teṉṉintiya moḻikaḷāyiṉuñ cari) ellāvaṟṟuḷḷum, cittiya moḻik kalappō, tirāviṭa moḻik kalappō perum pālum orē paṭittāṉatu. (2) āriyarāl mutalil kaippaṟṟap peṟṟa vaṭa intiyak kōṭṭaṅkaḷuḷ ak kalappiṉ aḷavu mikavuṅ kuṟukiyatu. (3) caṟṟuc cēymaiyiluḷḷa takkāṇam, teliṅkāṇam, maicūr ākiya iṭaṅkaḷil ak kalappiṉ aḷavu caṟṟup perukiyatu. (4) nila mukkiṉ teṉkōṭiyil iruppatum, maṉuviṉ kālattilum, irāmāyaṇa kālattilum pārppaṉa ātikkam kārt tikaip piṟaipōl iruntuvarappeṟṟatumākiya tamiḻ nāṭṭilō atu mikap parantu perukiyiruntatu.

mukaṟkaṇ, immuṭipu paṇṭaiya intiya varalāṟṟiṉ pōkkōṭu ottatākavē kāṇappaṭum. eṉiṉum, moḻi yārāyccik kaṇṇāṭikoṇṭu pārkkiṉ vaṭa intiyap paḻaṅkuṭi makkaḷum, teṉṉintiyap paḻaṅkuṭi makkaḷum orē iṉattār eṉṟu colliviṭuvataṟkillai. vaṭa intiya moḻikaḷiṉ ilakkaṇa amaippu cittiya moḻikaḷōṭu ottiruppatāṉālum, tirāviṭa moḻikaḷuṭaṉ ciṟappāka ottiruppatākak kāṇappaṭavillai. vaṭa intiya moḻikaḷuḷ kāṇap peṟum vaṭamoḻic cārpaṟṟa pakuti tirāviṭa moḻikaḷōṭu evvaḷavu toṭarpuṭaiyatākak kāṇappaṭukiṟatō, avvaḷaviṟkuk kīḻait turukkiya moḻikaḷuṭaṉō, aṉṟic cittiya moḻikaḷuṭaṉōṭu toṭar puṭaiyatākavuṅ kāṇappaṭukiṟatu. ātalāl mēṟkoḷkai eḷitil oppukkoḷḷak kūṭiyatoṉṟāka illai.

vaṭa intiya moḻikaḷiṉ ilakkaṇa amaippiṟkum, tirāviṭa moḻikaḷiṉ ilakkaṇa amaippiṟkum uḷḷa talaimaiyāṉa oppumaikaḷ kīḻvarupavaiyām:–

(1) peyarccoṟkaḷiṉpiṉ urupukaḷ cērntu vēṟṟumaikaḷ uṇṭātal. (2) paṉmaip peyarkaḷiṉ vēṟṟumai orumaiyiṉiṉṟum vēṟupaṭāmal paṉmai vikutiyai mutalil cērttup piṉ vēṟṟumai urupukaḷaic cērppataṉāl uṇṭātal. (3) vaṭa intiya moḻikaḷ palavaṟṟuḷ tirāviṭa moḻikaḷaip pōlavē taṉmaip paṉmaiyil muṉṉilaiyai uḷappaṭutti oṉṟum, vilakki oṉṟumāka iraṇṭu vaṭivaṅkaḷ iruttal. (4) urupukaḷum, oṭṭukkaḷum peyarukkumuṉ varāmal piṉvarutal. (5) viṉaic colliṉ kālattai iṭainilai mutaliya uṟuppukaḷ kāṭṭal. (6) taḻuvu vākkiyaṅkaḷ talaimai vākkiyattiṉ muṉ varutal. (7) taḻuvum moḻi taḻuvappaṭum moḻikkup piṉ varutal.

vaṭa intiya moḻikaḷ tirāviṭa moḻikaḷ ākiyavaṟṟiṉ ilakkaṇa amaippil ittakaiya oppumaikaḷ kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa eṉpatu uṇmaiyē. āṉāl ivaṟṟāl ivviraṇṭu moḻiyiṉaṅkaḷukkum nērāṉa uṟavu uḷa teṉak kūṟiviṭutaṟkillai. ēṉeṉil, iv voppumaikaḷ tirāviṭamoḻikaḷōṭumaṭṭum ciṟappākak kāṇappaṭum oṟṟumaika ḷākāmal, cittiya iṉattaic cērnta moḻiyiṉaṅkaḷ palavaṟṟōṭum, orē vakaiyil, orē aḷavil kāṇappaṭum potuppaṭaiyāṉa oppumaika ḷākiṉṟaṉa. turukkiya maṅkōliya moḻikaḷukkum, tirāviṭa moḻikaḷukkum iṭaiyē kāṇappaṭum vēṟṟumaikaḷil yātoṉṟum, vaṭa intiya moḻikaḷiṟ kāṇappaṭavillai. eṭuttuk kāṭṭāka, cittiya moḻiyiṉaṅkaḷilillāta peyareccattai eṭuttukkoḷvōm. itu kōṇṭu oṉṟu nīṅkalāka ēṉaiya tirāviṭa moḻikaḷ ellāvaṟṟilum uḷḷatu; āṉāl vaṭa intiya moḻikaḷ oṉṟilum illai.

ataip pōṉṟē, tirāviṭa moḻikaḷil ellā viṉaittiripukaḷukkum oḻuṅkāka etirmaṟaiviṉai irukkiṉṟatu. avaṟṟuḷ etuvum vaṭaintiya moḻikaḷil illai. tirāviṭa moḻikaḷiṟ kāṇappaṭum iṭappeyar eṇṇup peyarkaḷ mutaliyavaṟṟuḷ oṉṟutāṉum avaṟṟuḷ illai. āṉāl, intiyāvukku appāl pehistaṉ paṭṭayaṅkaḷilum, cīṉa moḻiyilum, ostiyakku[7] moḻiyilum, lāppar[8] moḻiyiluṅkūṭa ivaṟṟuṭ cila kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. mēlum, im moḻikaḷil tirāviṭat tākku etēṉum iruntirukkak kūṭumāyiṉ, kai, kāl, kaṇ, kātu (cevi) pōṉṟa mutaṉmaiyāṉa cila coṟkaḷēṉum, avaṟṟiṉ citaivukaḷēṉum ivaṟṟuḷ em moḻiyilāyiṉum kāṇappaṭāmalirukka iṭamillai. uṇmaiyil attakaiya ec collum ivaṟṟuḷ yāṇṭuṅ kāṇappaṭa villai. pērāciriyar sṭīveṉsaṉ kaṇṭa cila oppumaikaḷ aruvaḻakkāy eṅkō mūlaiyilirun teṭukkappaṭṭa coṟkaḷ ākum. vēṟu mutaṟcoṟkaḷum, ilakkaṇa amaiti oppumaikaḷum illāviṭattu ac ciṟupāṉmaiyāṉa oppumaikaḷaik koṇṭu oru muṭivukku varamuṭiyātu. ākavē, mutaṉmaiyāṉa vērc coṟkaḷaip poṟutta varaiyil tirāviṭa moḻikaḷukkum, vaṭa intiya moḻi kaḷukkumiṭaiyē ettuṇai vēṟupāṭu! āṉāl tirāviṭa moḻikaḷ ellāvaṟṟuḷḷum ettuṇai oṟṟumai! viyakkattakka iv vuṇmaikaḷaik kīḻvarum aṭṭavaṇaiyiṟ kāṇalām: taṉmai muṉṉilai yorumaip peyar, iṭap peyarkaḷuḷ eḻuvāyil or uruvum, piṟa vēṟṟumaikaḷil vēṟu uruvum, viṉaivikutikaḷil maṟṟōruruvumāka māṟi varuvatuṭaṉ, orē iṭattilum urukkaḷ paṇṭaik kālattil oruvakaiyākavum, piṟkālattil vēṟākavum irukkiṉṟamaiyāl vēṇṭiya iṭaṅkaḷil avai yāvum oppumaikkākat tarappaṭukiṉṟaṉa.

(piṉvarum aṭṭavaṉaikaḷil eḷitil oppiṭṭaṟitaṟkāka vaṭamoḻi iṭappeyarkaḷum, turukkiya iṭappeyarkaḷum piṟaikkuṟikaḷiṭṭu mutaliṟ koṭukkappeṟṟuḷḷaṉa.)

* * *





keḷara moḻikaḷiṉ iṭap peyarkaḷukkum tirāviṭa moḻikaḷiṉ iṭap peyarkaḷukkum malaikkum maṭuvukkumuḷḷa vēṟṟumai yiruppatai nōkka, 'am moḻikaḷiṉ tiripukku mūlakāraṇamāṉa tākkutalkaḷ vēṟevaiyāyiṉum ākakkūṭum, tirāviṭa moḻikaḷ ākā eṉpatu teṟṟeṉap pulappaṭukiṉṟatu. āṉāl, atē iṭap peyarkaḷiṉ ārāycciyālēyē pala vaṭamoḻikaḷilum cittiya vikutiyākiya ṉakaram iruppatu kāṇalām. ataṉōṭu, taṉmaiyil vaṭa intiya moḻikaḷ oṉṟilēṉum vaṭamoḻi eḻuvāyuru illāmal, turukkiya moḻiyiṉ eḻuvāyaip pōṉṟa mai(ṉ) allatu ma(ṉ) eṉṟa cittiya tōṟṟamuṭaiya uruvē nilavutal kāṇka. oruvēḷai etirkāla ārāyccikaḷāl tirāviṭa uṟavu uṇṭeṉṟu ēṟpaṭṭālum ēṟpaṭalām. eṉavē, vaṭa intiya moḻikaḷiṉ vaṭa moḻic cārpaṟṟa pakutiyum, teṉ intiya moḻikaḷum iraṇṭum āriyac cārpaṟṟavai eṉpatum, iraṇṭum cittiyac cārpuṭaiyavai eṉpatun tavira vēṟu evvakaiyilum oppumaiyuṭaiyaṉa alla eṉṟē tuṇiyat takum.

iṉi, ṭākṭar kuṇṭerṭ vaṭa intiya moḻikaḷil maṭṭumalla, vaṭa moḻiyilēkūṭa, tirāviṭat tākkuk kāṇappaṭukiṟatu eṉkiṟār.[1] inti moḻiyai ārāynta kireḷas eṉpavarō, vaṭamoḻic cārpaṟṟa pakuti yeṉṟoṉṟu avvaḷavā yiruntirukka vaḻiyillai eṉṟu kūṟi, ataṟkuk kīḻvarum kāraṇaṅkaḷaik kāṭṭukiṟār: (1) ammoḻiyiṉ paḻamaiyāṉa ilakkaṇa nūlār ataṉaik kuṟippiṭātatu. (2) vaṭa moḻic cārpaṟṟateṉa viraintu tuṇiyappaṭṭa coṟkaḷ, nuṇṇiya ārāycciyāl, vaṭa

* * *



↑ ТҺе Journal of the German Oriental Society for 1869.





moḻi yeṉṟu perumpālum niṟuvappaṭṭu varukiṉṟamai. (3) vaṭamoḻi vaḷarcciyuṟṟa kālattil, piṟamoḻit tākkutalkaḷāl vaṭa intiya moḻikaḷ tiripuṟṟamaikku eṉṉeṉṉa niyatikaḷ kāṇappaṭṭaṉavō anniyatikaḷai ikkāla vaṭanāṭṭu moḻikaḷum piṉpaṟṟivarukiṉṟamai. ic ceytiyaik kuṟittu 1872-l pīms eṉpār ”āriyac cārpaṟṟa ik kāla intiya moḻikaḷiṉ oppilakkaṇam”[1] eṉṟa nūlil piṉvarumāṟu viḷakkiyuḷḷār: ”iṉi, vaṭamoḻiyumallāta āriyamumallāta coṟkaḷaip paṟṟi ārāyvōm. āriyar intiyāvukku vantapōtu tamakkumuṉ aṅkuk kuṭiyirunta makkaḷōṭu neṭunāḷ pōriṭṭu nāṭṭai avarkaḷiṭamiruntu paṟittāka vēṇṭiyiruntatu. attakaiya pōrkaḷukkiṭaiyē amaiti nilaviya kālamum iruntiruttal vēṇṭum. appōtu iru tiṟattiṉarum kalantu oruvaruṭaṉ oruvar karuvip pōr ceyyātu, vāḻkkaip pōr naṭāttiyiruppar. āriyāllātā riṭaiyē āriyaratu tākkē mikutiyāyirun tiruppiṉum, āriyariṭaiyē āriyarallātār tākkum oraḷaviṟku illātirukka muṭiyātu. ākavē, vaṭamoḻiyilēyē āriyac cārpaṟṟaṉavākat tōṉṟum coṟkaḷ cila kāṇappaṭukiṉṟamai iyalpē. ik kāla vaṭa intiya moḻikaḷilō ic coṟkaḷ mikutiyākak kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. eṉavē, ec coṟkaḷ āriyac coṟkaḷākat tōṟṟavillaiyō avaiyellām āriyac cārpaṟṟavaiyē eṉṟu kūṟa muṉvaruvatu vaḻakkamākiviṭṭatu.

“iṅṅaṉam āriyarkaḷ paḻaṅkuṭikaḷiṭamiruntu kaṭaṉ vāṅkiṉar eṉṟa koḷkaiyai oppukkoḷvataṟkut

1.

taṭaikaḷ pala uḷḷaṉa. moḻiyiyaliṟ piṟa cāṉṟukaḷiṉ tuṇaiyillāviṭattu veṟum colloppumai koṇṭu etaiyum tuṇintuviṭa muṭiyātu. pala vaḻikaḷilum ciṟanta pōṟiñarkūṭa it tuṟaiyil, ”āṉaikkum nāṉku kāl, pūṉaikkum nāṉku kāl; āṉaik kālilum pūṉaik kālilum nakaṅkaḷ kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa” eṉṟa oppumaikaḷiṉ pēril muṭivukaṭṭiviṭukiṉṟaṉar. oru tamiḻc collil oru pakaram irukkiṟatu; oru vaṭacollilum oru pakaram irukkiṟatu. ākavē, vaṭacol tamiḻc colliliruntu vantatu eṉṟu kūṟiviṭuvatā! mutalāvatāka, intiyap paḻaṅkuṭikaḷaiviṭa uṭalvaliyilum aṟivuvaṉmaiyilum āriyarkaḷ uyarvuṭaiyavarkaḷākavē iruntaṉar. eṉavē, avarkaḷ ēṟṟataiviṭa īntatē mikutiyā yiruntirukka vēṇṭum. iraṇṭāvatāka, intiyāvukku varuvataṉ muṉṉarē āriyarkaḷ colvaḷa mikkatoru moḻiyai uṭaiyavarāyiruntaṉar. eṉavē uṭai, karuvikaḷ, taṭṭumuṭṭukaḷ, kaṉṟu kālikaḷ, uṭaṟ kūṟukaḷ, uṟavu muṟaikaḷ pōṉṟa potuk karuttukkaḷukkuriya coṟkaḷaikkūṭa avarkaḷ kaṭaṉvāṅka vēṇṭum nilaimai avarkaḷiṭam iruntirukkamuṭiyātu. avarkaḷ kaṭaṉ vāṅkavēṇṭu meṉṟāl putiya ceṭi koṭikaḷ, uyir vakaikaḷ, tām muṉṉark kaṇṭirāta putup poruḷkaḷ ākiyavaṟṟiṉ peyarkaḷaiyēyākum. ivaṟṟilkūṭat tam moḻic coṟkaḷaiyē tirittum kūṭṭiyum putuc coṟkaḷai ākkik koḷḷum āṟṟal ellā moḻikaḷukkum potuvāy amaintuḷḷatu. mūṉṟāvatāka, nilaiyalaip[2] poṟuttu eḻum cikkalkaḷ irukkiṉṟaṉa. āriyar varukaiyiṉpōtu avarkaḷuṭaṉ naṭpākavō pakaiyākavō uṟavupūṇṭa makkaḷ yār yār? vantēṟiya āriyarka ḷel lōrumē tirāviṭarkaḷuṭaṉ neruṅki uṟavāṭi irukka muṭiyumā? muṭiyumeṉṟāl eppoḻutu? eppaṭi? iṉṟaiya ārāycci nilaiyil iv viṉākkaḷukkut tiṟampaṭa viṭaiyiṟuppatu aritu. eṉiṉum, avaṟṟiṟku etāvatu oru vaḻi kāṇal vēṇṭum. kaṅkaik karaiyiṉiṉṟum āriyarāl teṟkē viraṭṭappaṭṭa makkaḷ kōlēriyarum, vaṭakkē viraṭṭappaṭṭavar tipēttuk kalappumakkaḷum āvar. eṉavē, kōlēriyar moḻic cāṉṟiṉṟi vaṭa moḻic col oṉṟu nērākat tamiḻiliruntu peṟappaṭṭu viṭṭatu eṉpatai oppuk koḷḷutal cālātu. ittakaiya nilaimaiyil ac colliṉ mūlattai ārāyum pakuti uṇmaiyil vaṭamoḻiyum, ataṉ kiḷaimoḻikaḷum eṉṟa aḷavukkē kuṟukiviṭu meṉpataṟkaiyamillai. āḻntu nōkkiṉāl atil perumpālum ataṟkuriya vērccollaik kāṇalām.”

ik kūṟṟu oruvāṟu oppukkoḷḷak kūṭiyatēyām. tirāviṭa moḻikaḷaip paṟṟiyavarai vaṭa intiya moḻikaḷil avaṟṟiṉ tākkutal oruciṟitutāṉ iruntirukkak kūṭum. āṉāl, cittiyat tākkutalkaḷālō aṉṟi āriyac cārpaṟṟa piṟa moḻikaḷiṉ tākkutalkaḷālō, vaṭa intiya moḻikaḷ iṉṟaiya tirinta nilaiyai aṭaintirukkak kūṭāvō eṉpatu vēṟu kēḷvi. tirāviṭam, cittiyam, āriyac cārpaṟṟavai eṉpaṉa oruporuṭ kiḷavikaḷalla. pīms eṉpār ’uyir olimāṟṟaṅkaḷ'[3] eṉṟa pirivil, ”āriya uyirolikaḷ intiyāvukku vaṭakkilum kiḻakkilum naṭuvilum teṟkilum uḷḷa veḷi iṉattārāl evvaḷavu māṟutalaṭaintaṉaveṉṟu collamuṭiyātu. āṉāl, māṟutal aṭaintuḷḷaṉa eṉpatu maṭṭum maṟukkamuṭiyāta uṇmaiyākum” eṉṟu kūṟiyuḷḷār. vaṭa intiya moḻikaḷuḷ akaramum ākāramum ēkāramāka māṟiyamai, ittakaiya āriyac cārpaṟṟa māṟutal eṉṟu avar kūṟukiṟār. nāvaṭi aṇṇa meyyākiya ḷakarattaip paṟṟik kūṟukaiyil, atu rakara ṭakaraṅkaḷuṭaṉ mayaṅkukiṉṟatu eṉṟum, atu naṭu intiyāviluḷḷa kōlarkaḷiṉ kuḻuvil kāṇappaṭukiṟatu eṉṟum avar uraikkiṉṟār. ik kōlarkaḷuṭaṉum tirāviṭarkaḷuṭaṉum neṭunāḷ uṟavu koṇṭirunta marāṭṭiya, oriyā makkaḷiṭai, inta ḷakaram mikutiyākap payilvatu viyappaṉṟu eṉṟum avar kūṟukiṟār.

“cinti moḻiyiṉ ilakkaṇam”[4] eṉṟa nūlil ṭākṭar erṉasṭu ṭrampu āriyac cārpaṟṟa allatu tāttāriya moḻiyiṉat tākkutalkaḷ vaṭa intiya moḻikaḷil kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa’’ eṉṟu uṟutiyākak kūṟukiṟār; muṉ nā aṇpal oliyākiya takara nakara skaraṅkaḷiṉiṭamāka, avai nāaṭi aṇṇa oliyākiya ṭakara ṇakara ṣakaraṅkaḷaip peritum ēṟkiṉṟamai ataṟkuc cāṉṟu pakarum eṉṟum kūṟukiṟār. atē pōṉṟu uyirppu oliyākiya ha eṉpataip pirākiruta moḻi vilakkuvatu nōkka, “ataip pēciya potu makkaḷiṭaiyē tāttāriyat tākku iruntirukkavēṇṭum. teṟkēyuḷḷa tirāviṭa moḻikaḷil iv eḻuttu illāmai kāṇka” eṉṟum kūṟukiṟār. mēlum, tirāviṭa moḻiyiṉattaic cērnta teluṅku ‘cca’, ‘ja’ eṉṟa eḻuttukkaḷai ‘ṭca’ eṉṟum, ṭs eṉṟum olippatu pōlavē marāṭṭi moḻiyum olikkiṉṟatu; ātalāl tirāviṭat tākku marāṭṭiyiluḷḷatu veḷippaṭai eṉṟum avar muṭivukaṭṭukiṟār.

﻿e. tirāviṭa moḻikaḷ enta iṉattaic cērntavai?



﻿ tirāviṭa moḻikaḷ cittiya moḻiyiṉattōṭu cērttaṟkuriyaṉavē yaṉṟi intu-airōppiya moḻi yiṉattōṭu cērttaṟkuriyaṉavākā eṉpatu pērāciriyar rāsk[1] eṉpāriṉ muṭipākum. cērttaṟkuriyaṉa eṉṟu maṭṭum kūṟalāmē yaṉṟi, cērntaṉa eṉṟu colliviṭu taṟkillai; avai cittiya moḻikaḷōṭu toṭarpuṭaiyaṉa vēyaṉṟic cittiya moḻiyiṉattiliruntu peṟappaṭṭavai yalla. cittiya moḻiyiṉam eṉpatil piṉṉiṣ, turukki, maṅkōliyam, tuṅkūcasiyam ākiya moḻiyiṉaṅkaḷ aṭaṅkum. rāsk eṉpārē ivvāṟu mutaṉmutal vakaippaṭuttiṉār. im moḻikaḷ aṉaittum orē ilakkaṇa amaippuṭai yavaiyāy, orēpaṭittāṉa potu vitikaḷaiyum piṉpaṟṟukiṉṟaṉa. urupukaḷ allatu tuṇaic coṟkaḷaic cērppataṉālēyē avai ilakkaṇa vēṟupā ṭuṟukiṉṟaṉa; cemittiya[2] moḻiyiṉaṅkaḷilō pakutiyiṉ uyirolikaḷai māṟṟuvatāl vēṟupā ṭuṟukiṉṟaṉa; cīṉattilum, piṟa taṉippaṭṭa ōracai moḻikaḷilum coṟṟōṭariluḷḷa coṟkaḷai muṉṉum piṉṉum māṟṟi yamaippatāl vēṟupāṭukiṉṟaṉa. ivvakaiyil intu-airōppiya moḻikaḷum cittiya moḻikaḷaip pōlavē mutaṉmutal, aṭukkiyal[3] muṟaiyaiyē kaiyāṇṭiruntaṉa eṉṟu tōṟṟukiṉṟaṉa. āṉāl avaṟṟiṉ aṭaikaḷ allatu tuṇaic coṟkaḷ varavarat tēyntu vikutikaḷākavō urupukaḷākavō māṟiviṭṭamaiyāl avai cittiya iṉaṅ kaḷaip pōlavumiṉṟi, cīṉa moḻikaḷaip pōlavumiṉṟi taṉippaṭṭa oruvakai iṉamāyiṉa. cittiya moḻikaḷaic cilar tāttār moḻiyiṉam eṉpar; piṉṉiṣ iṉam, āltāyik iṉam, maṅkōliya iṉam, turāṉiya iṉam eṉṟum palavāṟākap palar kūṟuvar. āṉāl ivaiyaṉaittum ip peru moḻik kuḻuviluḷḷa moḻiyiṉaṅkaḷ palavaṟṟuḷ oṉṟiraṇṭaiyē taṉittaṉikkuṟikkumātalāl avaṟṟaip peru moḻik kuḻuviṉ peyarākak koḷḷal cālātu. itukāṟum, paṇṭaik kirēkka aṟiñarkaḷāl cittiyam eṉṟa peyar potuppaṭa āciyāvilum, airōppāvilum iruntuvanta nākarikamaṟṟa paṇṭai makkaḷaik kuṟikkavē payaṉpaṭuttap peṟṟuvantuḷḷamaiyāl ataṉaip peyarākavē vaḻaṅkutalē cālpuṭaittām.

pēraṟiñar rāsk tirāviṭa moḻikaḷ cittiyac cārpuṭaiyavai eṉak kūṟiṉavarkaḷuḷ mutalvarāyiṉum, ataṉait teḷivupaṭutti viḷakka ettakaiya muyaṟciyum avar ceyyavillai. avaraip piṉpaṟṟiya palarum atēmātiri avar kūṟiyataik kūṟiṉarēyaṉṟi vēṟu oṉṟum ceyya villai. piriccārṭu[4] eṉpār tam ārāyccikaḷil cittiya tirāviṭa moḻikaḷiṉ cila potuppaṭaiyāṉa oṟṟumaikaḷai oṉṟiraṇṭu ilakkaṇac cārpāṉa eṭuttukkāṭṭukaḷuṭaṉ, viḷakkip pōyiṉar. ātāraṅkaḷuṭaṉ āyntu viḷakkuvatu eḷitaṉṟu. ēṉeṉil aṅṅaṉam viḷakka vēṇṭumāyiṉ mutaṉ mutalil tirāviṭa moḻikaḷait taṉittaṉi ārāyntu avaṟṟiṉ oppumaiyiṉmūlam avaṟṟiṉ paṇṭaiya urukkaḷaiyum ilakkaṇa amaitikaḷaiyum talaimaiyāṉa paṇpukaḷaiyum aṟivatōṭu, atēmātirip piṟa cittiya uṭpirivukaḷiṉ paṇpukaḷaiyum uṇarntāka vēṇṭum. ulaka varalāṟṟiṉ potuppaṭaiyāṉa amaitikaḷ”eṉṟa tokutiyil māksmūlaratu turaṉiyamoḻikaḷ paṟṟiya namatu iṉṟaiya aṟivunilai” eṉṟa ārāycciyurai piṉṉar veḷivantatu. annūlmikac ciṟantatoṉṟu. eṉiṉum, annūlil āciriyar tām eṭuttukkoṇṭa poruḷ muḻuvataiyum, potuppaṭavē ārāyntuḷḷār. ataṉāl īṇṭu eṭuttukkoṇṭa poruḷukku atu putuccāṉṟukaḷ oṉṟaiyum pakaravillai. tirāviṭa moḻikaḷiṉ ilakkaṇa amaitikaḷum, coṟṟoṭar muṟaiyum mēmpōkkākap pārppavarkkuk kūṭa viḷaṅkak kūṭumāyiṉum, avaṟṟiṉ olimuṟai, avaṟṟiṉ iṭavakai māṟṟaṅkaḷ, vaḻakkukaḷ, ilakkaṇa urumāṟṟaṅkaḷ mutaliyavai taṉippaṭṭa ārāycciyālaṉṟi viḷaṅkat takkavaiyalla.

iṉi, ivvakai ārāycciyait toṭaṅkumuṉ pōṭliṅ eṉpār varaiyaṟuttuk kūṟiya, piṉvarum karuttu niṉaivu kūraṟpāṟṟu : “ tām naṉku ārāyntaṟiyāta moḻikaḷaippaṟṟi oruvar eḻuta muṟpaṭutal tīṅku viḷaippatākum. cittiya moḻiyaippaṟṟi ārāyavēṇṭumāṉāl, avaṟṟuḷḷaṭaṅkiya palvēṟu moḻikaḷiṉ oppiyalkaḷum vēṟṟumaiyiyalkaḷum naṉku terintiruttal vēṇṭum. pālar’ eṉpār eḻutiya piṉṉiya moḻiyārāyccik kaṭṭuraikaḷum, pērāciriyar haṉḵpalvi' eṉpār eḻutiya turāṉiya moḻiyārāyccikaḷum ittuṟaiyil pērutavi puriya vallaṉavām. haṅkēriyaṉ, vōkul" oskiyāk, piṉṉiṣ ākiya nāṉku moḻikaḷum orē potu moḻiyiṉattaic cērntavai eṉṟum, avaṟṟiṉ

1. “On the present state of our knowledge of the Turanian Languages” by Max Muller in Bunsen's “Outlines of the Philosophy of Üniversal History. 2. Bohtlingh. 3. Bollar. 4. Prof. Hunfalvy.5. Vogul. ilakkaṇaṅkaḷellām neruṅkiya toṭarpuṭaiyaṉa eṉṟum haṉḵpalvi viḷakkiyuḷḷār.

pēraṟiñar pāṭ, piraṭerik mullar[5] mutaliya pala moḻiyiyalār cittiya allatu turāṉiya moḻikaḷ oru potu moḻiyiliruntu vantavai eṉṟu uṟutiyākak kūṟamuṭiyātu eṉṟu karutukiṉṟaṉar. pēraṟiñar piḷākkum[6] turāṉiya moḻikaḷ palavum orupuṭai orē paṭittāṉa vaḷarcciyai aṭaintuḷḷa orē kuḻu moḻikaḷō allatu pala kuḻu moḻikaḷō ākum” eṉak karutukiṉṟaṉar. eṉiṉum potuppaṭa nōkka, kastrēṉ[7] mutaliya aṟiñar kūṟukiṉṟapaṭi avaṟṟiṉ oṟṟumaikaḷ moḻikku iṉṟiyamaiyātavaiyākavum, palavākavum irukkiṟapaṭiyāl avaṟṟuḷ ēṟpaṭum toṭakkap potumaiyai, aḵtāvatu orē mutaṉmoḻiyiṉiṉṟu avai toṭaṅkiyirukka vēṇṭum eṉṟa muṭivai, ēṟkātirukka muṭiyātu. immoḻikaḷiṉ col oṟṟumaikaḷum vaṭiva oṟṟumaikaḷum āriya cemikkiya iṉaṅkaḷiṟ kāṇappaṭum oṟṟumaiyiṉiṉṟum vēṟupaṭṭavai yāyiṉum, taṟceyalāy ēṟpaṭak kūṭiyavai alla, orē mutaṉmoḻiyiliruntu ēṟpaṭṭavaiyāvē irukka vēṇṭum eṉpatu teḷivu” eṉpar aṟiñar māksmūlarum, mēlum neṭunāḷ pirintiyaṅkiya aṭukkiyal moḻikaḷuḷ, moḻiyiyaṟkaiyiṉ aṭippaṭaip pakutikaḷ "allatu māṟupāṭu ceyyamuṭiyāta col vakaikaḷāṉa iṭappeyarkaḷ, eṇṇup peyarkaḷ, urupukaḷ pōṉṟavaṟṟait tavira vēṟu ettakaiya oṟṟumaikaḷaiyum nām etirpārkka muṭiyātu. mēṟpaṭaiyāṉa poruḷ koṇṭa coṟkaḷuḷ vēṟṟumai iruppataiviṭa, oṟ ṟumai yiruppataik tāṉ nām arumaiyāyk koḷḷa vēṇṭum eṉṟum avar kūṟukiṟār.

mēlē tirāviṭa moḻikaḷ vaṭamoḻiyiṉiṉṟum vēṟupaṭṭavai eṉpataṟkuk kāṭṭiya akkāraṇaṅkaḷaiyē avai cikkiya iṉaṅkaḷuṭaṉ oṉṟupaṭṭavai eṉpataṟkum kāṭṭalākum. cikkiya iṉattuṭpaṭṭa moḻikaḷ aṉaittum orē iṉam eṉpataiyum ak kāraṇaṅkaḷē kāṭṭum. avaṟṟiṉ viḷakkattaik kuṟainta aḷavil eṭuttuk koṇṭāluṅkūṭa avai toṭarpu eṉpatu aiyamaṟat teḷivākum. cila kūṟukaḷil maṭṭum tirāviṭa moḻikaḷ intu - airōppiya moḻikaḷai oṭ ṭiyavaiyāyk kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa; aḵtāvatu oraḷavukku, ' aṭukkiyal moḻiyāy iruppatiliruntu muṉṉēṟi (intu-airōppiya moḻikaḷaip pōla) c citaiviyal' moḻikaḷ ākiyuḷḷaṉa. vēṟṟumaiyilum viṉaittiripilum payaṉpaṭum tuṇaic coṟkaḷiṟ pala taṉi moḻiyāka vaḻakkāṟaṟṟuppōyiṉa. āṉal iḵtoṉṟai muṉṉiṭṭu, tirāviṭa moḻikaḷaic cittiya iṉattiliruntu pirikka vēṇṭiyatillai; ēṉeṉil ittuṇaiccoṟkaḷ ciṟcila iṭaṅkaḷil muṟṟilum urupukaḷ allatu vikutikaḷākak kuṟaintuviṭṭaṉavāyiṉum, coṟkaḷiṉ pakutikaḷiliruntu ivai eppoḻutum pirikkappaṭak kūṭiyaṉavākavē iruntuvarukiṉṟaṉa; intu-airōppiya moḻikaḷiṟ pōṉṟu ivai pakutiyuṭaṉ oṉṟupaṭṭu orē moḻiyiṉ kūṟāyviṭavillai. intu - airōppiya moḻikaḷil ivai aṅṅaṉam kalantu viṭṭapaṭiyāl pakuti etu, vikuti etu eṉṟu pirippatu kūṭa aritāy ṉaviṭukiṟatu. ataṉēṭu, turukkiyam, piṉṉiyam, haṅkēri

moḻiyiyalil cīṉattiṉ iṭam” eṉṟa nūlil eṭkiṉs cikkiya iṉattiṉ orumai, avaṟṟuṭaṉ tirāviṭa moḻikaḷiṉ toṭarpu ākiya koḷkaikaḷaip peritum ēṟṟuk koḷḷukiṉṟār. avar kāṭṭum pala taṟceyalāṉa, nuṇukkamāṉa oṟṟumaikaḷ āḻntu ārāyumaḷavil nilainiṟkā. āyiṉum potuppaṭak kūṟumaḷavil ittuṟaiyil oruvar ārāyuntōṟum tirāviṭa moḻikaḷ maṅkōliya moḻikaḷ ākiya iraṇṭum oruvāṟu orumaippāṭuṭaiyaṉa eṉpataik kāṇpar. tirāviṭa cīṉa moḻikaḷiṭai oṟṟumaikaḷ mikuti eṉak kūṟa muṭiyātu. eṭkiṉs cittiya iṉammaṭṭumaṉṟu, āciya airōppiya moḻikaḷ aṉaittumē orē mutaṉmoḻiyiṉ vakait tiripukaḷ ākum ” eṉkiṟār. ituvarai maṉita vakuppiṉ moḻikaḷai aṟiñar ārāynta aḷavil ikkōṭpāṭu poruttamākat tōṉṟiṉum aṟiviyal muṟaippaṭi itu muṟṟilum nilaināṭṭappaṭa iṉṉum neṭunāḷ cellak kūṭum. kaṭaiciyāka, tirāviṭa moḻikaḷiṉ amaippukaḷai viḷakkac cittiya moḻiyiṉattai oṟṟumaippaṭutti ārāynta aṟivu evvaḷavō payaṉpaṭukiṟatu eṉṟa oru ceytiyē avaṟṟuḷ cittiyat toṭarpu irukkiṟatu eṉpataṟkōr aṟikuṟiyākum. itaṟku eṭuttuk kāṭṭākat tirāviṭa moḻiyiluḷḷa viṉaiccoṟkaḷiṉ peyareccattiṉ amaippai eṭuttukkoḷḷalām. maṅkōliyam, mañcu ivaṟṟil iḵtu eṅṅaṉam amaintuḷḷatu eṉpatai aṟintapiṉṉarē, tirāviṭa moḻikaḷil eṅṅaṉam amaintuḷḷatu eṉpatai eḷitil aṟiyamuṭintatu. aṅṅaṉam aṟinta viḷakkattai ituvarai yārum maṟukkavō allatu vēṟu vakaiyil viḷakkavō illai eṉpatu kuṟippiṭattakkatu.

cittiya iṉaccārpuk koḷkaiyai uṟutippaṭuttum vakaiyil maṟṟoru cāṉṟuṅ kiṭaittuḷḷatu. atuvē mēṟku mīṭiyā allatu pārasīkattil uḷḷa pehistaṉ allatu pakistāṉ aṭṭavaṇaikaḷiṉ moḻipeyarppukkaḷ ākum. iv aṭṭavaṇaikaḷ ṭarayas hisṭāspas" eṉpavar tammaippaṟṟik tāmē eḻutiya varalāṟu ākum. ivai paḻam pāracīka moḻiyilum, pāpilōṉiya moḻiyilum, pārasīka, mīṭiyap pēraraciluḷḷa cittiya makkaḷum aṟiyum paṭiyāka avarkaḷatu tāymoḻiyilum eḻutappaṭṭirukkiṉṟaṉa. ivaṟṟiṉ moḻipeyarppu tirāviṭa cittiya uṟavai viḷakkum vakaiyil oru puttoḷiyāy viḷaṅkukiṉṟatu. ivaṟṟiṉ iraṇṭām tokuti teḷivākac cittiyac cārpāṉatu eṉṟu nāris’ tamatu kaṭṭuraiyil eḻutukiṟār. pēraṟiñar ālparṭ immoḻiyaip pēciya makkaḷ mīṭiyarē eṉṟu kūṟukiṟār. āyiṉum avarum immoḻi cittiya allatu turāṉiyac cārpāṉateṉṟē koḷkiṟār. itaṉāl, ki. mu.5-m nūṟṟāṇṭilēyē pēcappaṭṭa oru cikkiya iṉa moḻiyuṭaṉ tirāviṭa moḻikaḷai oṟṟumaippaṭuttum or ariya vāyppuk kiṭaittuḷḷatu. immoḻi potuppaṭac cittiya iṉaccārpuṭaiyatē. eṉiṉum, avaṟṟuṭ ciṟappāka ukrōpiṉṉiṣ kuḻuvuṭaṉ iṉṉum neruṅkiya toṭarpuṭaiyatākavuṅ kāṭṭappaṭukiṟatu. tirāviṭamoḻi

Prof. Oppert. kaḷ inta, ukrō - piṉṉiṣ moḻikaḷaic ciṟappāka ottirukkiṉṟaṉa eṉpatu iṅkē kūṟavēṇṭiyatoṉṟākum.tirāviṭa moḻikaḷukkum, ip pehistaṉ aṭṭavaṇaiyiṉ moḻikkum iṭaiyē kāṇappaṭum oṟṟumaikaḷ varumāṟu : ---

(i) iv aṭṭavaṇaikaḷil ṭ ṭa, ṭa, ṇa eṉṟa nā aṭi aṇṇa olikaḷ uḷḷaṉa. itē olikaḷ tirāviṭa moḻikaḷilum uḷḷaṉa. vaṭamoḻiyilum ivai uḷḷaṉavāyiṉum (piṟa āriya moḻikaḷuḷ illāmaiyai nōkkak) tirāviṭa moḻikaḷaip piṉpaṟṟi vaṭamoḻiyil ēṟpaṭṭa olikaḷē ivai eṉa ūkikkalām. nārisum ikkaruttaiyē veḷiyiṭṭuḷḷār.

(2) tirāviṭa moḻikaḷuḷ tamiḻaippōla, iv aṭṭavaṇai moḻikaḷuḷ valleḻuttukkaḷ moḻimutalil varumpoḻutum iraṭṭitta iṭattum valloli uṭaiyavaiyākavum, moḻi iṭaiyē uyirkaḷiṉ naṭuvē varum poḻutu aṇmai allatu arai aṅkāppu oliyuṭaiya ṉavākavum olikkiṉṟaṉa.

(3) ivvaṭṭavaṇai moḻiyiṉ āṟum vēṟṟumai urupukaḷ ṉa, ṉiṉa, iṉṉa eṉpavaiyākum. ivaṟṟiṟkuc cariyāṉa tirāviṭa urupukaḷ teluṅku ṉī eṉpatum, kōṇṭu allatu pirākuvi ṉa a eṉpavaiyum, tamiḻ iṉ eṉpatum ākum.

(4) aṭṭavaṇaikaḷil nāṉkām vēṟṟumai urupu ikki allatu ikka eṉpatākum. itaṉaiyokka urupukaḷ tāttār-turukkiyak kuḻuvilum, ukriyaṉ iṉattilum uḷa. āṉāl itaṉai muṟṟilum ottiruppavai tirāviṭa urupukaḷākiya ku, ki, ka eṉpavaiyām. tamiḻ, malaiyāḷa moḻikaḷil ivvurupukaḷ iraṭṭikkumpōtu avaṟṟiṉ muṉ u allatu i collolic cāriyaiyāka varukiṉṟatu. aṭṭavaṇaikaḷil uṉakku eṉṉum poruḷil niṉikka(niṉ -ikka) eṉṉum colluṭaṉ kaṉṉaṭam niṉake (niṉ-a-ke) malaiyāḷam niṉakku (niṉ-a-kku) eṉpavaṟṟai oppiṭṭu nōkkuka.

(5) aṭṭavaṇaikaḷiluḷḷa iṭappeyarkaḷiṉ iraṇṭām vēṟṟumai urupu uṉ, iṉ, ṉ eṉpavaiyām. ittuṭaṉ teluṅku urupu ṉu, ṉi eṉpataiyum, kaṉṉaṭam am, aṉṉu (aṉṉ-u) eṉpataiyum oppiṭṭu nōkkuka. (6) aṭṭavaṇaikaḷil varum eṇṇup peyarkaḷil eḻuttil eḻutiya peyar oṉṟu eṉṉum poruḷil varum kir eṉṟa col maṭṭumēyām. iḵtu uṇmaiyil peyaruḷiccol vaṭivattilēyē (aḵtāvatu paṇput tokaiyākavē) eḻutappaṭṭiruttal vēṇṭum. teluṅkil ivvaṭivam oka-eṉpatum, tamiḻil or-eṉpatum ākum. ku-moḻiyil tamiḻ oru eṉpataṟkuc cariyāṉa col ra eṉpatām. ik ku moḻiyiṉ vaṭivattiṟku aṭṭavaṇaiyiluḷḷa piṟitoru vaṭivamākiya ra allatu irra mikavum neruṅkiyatākum.

aṭṭavaṇaiyil eṇṇup peyarkaḷ peyaraṭaiyākum poḻutu im eṉṟa cāriyaiyaik koḷḷukiṉṟaṉa. itu tamiḻ ām eṉpataiyum sāmōyiyat im eṉpataiyum niṉaivūṭṭukiṉṟatu.

(7) tirāviṭa moḻikaḷilum, aṭṭavaṇaiyilum muṉṉilaip peyar oṉṟē. ivai iraṇṭilum eḻuvāy nī; piṟavēṟṟumaikku muṉṉaiya mutal vaṭivu niṉ eṉpatākum. tamakkuk kīḻppaṭiyiluḷḷavarkaḷai aḻaikka muṉṉilai orumaiyaiyē vaḻaṅkivanta kāraṇattāl ivvaṭṭavaṇaikaḷil muṉṉilaippaṉmai kāṇappaṭavillai. (8) aṭṭavaṇai moḻiyilum tirāviṭa moḻikaḷaip pōlavē peyareccam irukkiṟatu.āṉāl atōṭukūṭavē atil iṇaippu iṭappeyar oṉṟum kāṇappaṭukiṟatu. ākum. ippakuti pāracīkattiṉiṉṟum moḻipeyarkkappaṭṭatātalāl pāracīkattiluḷḷa iṇaippu iṭappeyaraittāṉ iṅkē puṉaintu uṇṭupaṇṇiyirukkavēṇṭum eṉṟu nāris karutukiṟār. aṭṭavaṇaiyiṉ peyareccam uruvil tirāviṭap peyareccattiṉiṉṟum vēṟupaṭṭiruppiṉum ataṉ poruḷum payaṉum tirāviṭa vaḻakkai ottē irukkiṉṟaṉa. ippeyareccamē cikkiya iṉac cārpuṭaiya moḻikaḷ aṉaittiṟkum potuvāṉa ciṟappup paṇpu eṉak koḷḷalām pōlum!



(9) aṭṭavaṇaiyiṉ ēval etirmaṟai muṭipu iṉṉi, kōṇṭil itu miṉṉi ākum.

ivvoṟṟumaikaḷ nīṅkalākap piṟavēṟṟumait tiripukaḷil pehistaṉ aṭṭavaṇaiyiṉ moḻi haṅkēriyam, mōrṭuviṉ mutaliya piṟa ukriya moḻikaḷai ottirukkiṉṟatu. āṉāl, ivvakaikaḷilellām tirāviṭa moḻikaḷ vēṟupaṭukiṉṟaṉa. avaṟṟiṉ viṉaiccollākkam cikkalaṟṟatu. avai perumpālum kālaṅkāṭṭum iṭainilaikaḷuṭaṉum, avaṟṟiṉpiṉ, pāl, iṭam kāṭṭa avaṟṟiṟkuriya iṭappeyarkaḷiṉ tiripukaḷaiyē koṇṭum viḷaṅkukiṉṟaṉ. ivvakaiyil vēṟṟumaikaḷ iruntumkūṭa, mēṟkūṟiya oṟṟumaikaḷ iṉṟiyamaiyāc ciṟappuṭaiyaṉa ātaliṉ, tirāviṭa moḻikaḷukkum cittiya moḻikaḷukkum iṭaiyiluḷḷa uṟavu oruciṟitēyāyiṉum aḵtu aṭippaṭaiyāṉatē eṉpatil aiyamirukka muṭiyātu. ēṉeṉil, ivvaḷavu vēṟṟumaikaḷukkiṭaiyilum iṉṟiyamaiyāta oṟ

iṅṅaṉam ilakkaṇa amaippu mutaliya pala oṟṟumaip paṇpukaḷiṉ mūlam tirāviṭa moḻikaḷ cittiyak cārpuṭaiyavai eṉṟu koḷḷappaṭiṉum, avai intu - airōppiya moḻiyiṉac cārpum uṭaiyavaiyē eṉpatu viyakkattakka cila oppumaikaḷāl uyttuṇarappaṭum. avaṟṟiṉ cittiyat toṭarpukūṭat taṉippaṭat turukkiyak kuḻuvuṭaṉē, ukriya, maṅkōliya, tuṅkūsiya moḻikaḷuṭaṉē aṉṟu ; ammoḻikaḷ aṉaittum tammuḷ evvaḷavō vēṟṟumaiyuṭaiyaṉa ; āyiṉum avaiyaṉaittiṟkum, tāyāyuḷḷa oru pēriṉa moḻiyuṭaṉēyē tirāviṭa moḻiyiṉaṅkaḷ toṭarpuṭaiyavai yām. ivaṟṟuḷ tirāviṭattiṟku mikavum aṇmaiyuṭaiyateṉak koḷḷakkūṭiyatu piṉṉiṣ allatu ukriyamē. oskiyakkuṭaṉum cila ciṟappāṉa uṟavukaḷ uḷḷaṉa. ilakkaṇa oppumai,col tokuti oppumai ākiyavaṟṟiṉ mūlamākamaṭṭum ārāyntu kaṇṭa immuṭipu pehistaṉ aṭṭavaṇaikaḷiṉ veḷiyiṭṭāl peritum uṟutiyaṭaivatu kāṇka. itaṉāl mīṭō-pāracīkaratu paṭaiyeṭuppiṟku

muṉ naṭu āciyāvil kuṭiyirunta makkaḷ turukkiya allatu maṅkōliyak kuḻuviṉar allar, ukriyak kuḻuviṉarē eṉpatu viḷaṅkum. veppa maṇṭalattuḷ irukkum roviṭak kuḻu eṅkē, vaṭa turuvap pakkattilirukkum piṉṉiyar, oskiyakkar, ukriyar eṅkē! ivarkaḷiṭaiyē ettaikaiya toṭarpu?

kirāviṭa moḻikaḷaic cittiyac cārpuṭaiyavai eṉṟu kaṇittaṟkāṉa teḷivukaḷai ārāykaiyil niṉaivil vaikka vēṇṭiyatoṉṟuṇṭu. aḵtāvatu, orupuṟam cittiya iṉa moḻikaḷiṉ aṭippaṭaiyāṉa toṭakka iyal’ paṇpukaḷ teḷivāṉavuruvuṭaiyavai āka iruppiṉum, iṉṉorupuṟam col tokutiyilum, vēṟu pala ciṟu kuṟippukkaḷilum oṉṟukkoṉṟu malaikkum maṭuvukkum uḷḷa vēṟṟumai uṭaiyavaiyāyirukkiṉṟaṉa eṉpatē. uṇmaiyil, intu - airōppiya iṉa moḻikaḷ oṉṟukku oṉṟu vēṟṟumaippaṭuvataiviṭa, cittiya moḻikaḷ oṉṟukkoṉṟu ettaṉaiyō maṭaṅku mikunta vēṟṟumaiyuṭaiyaṉa eṉṟu kūṟutal vēṇṭum. eṭuttukkāṭṭāka, intu - airōppiya moḻikaḷ aṉaittilum eṇkaḷiṉ peyarkaḷ ottirukkiṉṟaṉa eṉṟu coṉṉāṟ pōtātu, (vaṭamoḻi oṉṟu eṉṉum poruḷkoṇṭa eka eṉpatu nīṅkalāka maṟṟavai aṉaittum) orē vaṭivuṭaiyavaiyākavē irukkiṉṟaṉa eṉṟuṅ kūṟutal vēṇṭum. itaṟku nērmāṟāka, cittiya iṉa moḻikaḷil aṉaittiṟkum potuvāṉa oṟṟumaikaḷ arukiyirukkiṉṟaṉa eṉṟu coṉṉum pōtātu; ētēṉum iraṇṭu moḻikaḷukkup potuvāṉa eṇkaḷiṉ peyarkaḷkūṭa mika māṟupaṭṭē yirukkiṉṟaṉa eṉṟuṅ kūṟutal vēṇṭum. ivvēṟṟumaikaḷiṉ mikutippāṭṭai nōkkiṉ, intac cittiya iṉam intu - airōppiya iṉattaip pōl oru taṉi iṉam eṉṟu colvataiviṭap pala iṉaṅkaḷai uṭkoṇṭatōr pēriṉam eṉṟu colvatē cālum. ippēriṉattuṭpaṭṭa kuḻukkaḷiṉ orumaippāṭu nuṇukkamāṉa oṟṟumaikaḷāl niṟuvakkūṭiyataṉṟu, perumpōkkāṉa vaṭivu allatu amaippu oṟṟumaikaḷiṉālēyē niṟuvappaṭum. eṉavē, intu airōppiya iṉattuṭpaṭṭa moḻikaḷākiya vaṭamoḻi, sentu (kīḻap paḻam pārasīkam), paḻampārasīkam, kirēkkam ilattīṉam, kātik littuvēṉiyam, slavōṉiyam, kelttiyam mutaliya ovvoṉṟum ōriṉattuṭpaṭṭa taṉi moḻikaḷēyām. āṉāl cittiyap pēriṉattilē ivaṟṟilum mikunta vēṟṟumaikaḷaiyuṭaiya ciṟṟiṉa moḻikaḷ aintāṟukku mēṟpaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. ivvuṭpiriviṉaṅkaḷ ovvoṉṟilum kiṭṭattaṭṭa intu - airōppiya moḻikaḷil ettaṉait taṉi moḻikaḷ uṇṭō attaṉait taṉi moḻikaḷ uḷḷaṉa. ivaipōka vakaippaṭutti innāḷ varai iṉamākac cērkka muṭiyāta nilaiyuṭaiya irupatu muppatu taṉimoḻikaḷ kūṭa uḷḷaṉa.

intu - airōppiya iṉattiṟkum cittiyap pēriṉattiṟkum iṭaiyil uḷḷa ipperu vēṟṟumaikkuk kāraṇam mutaṉmutaṟ koṇṭē muṉṉaiya iṉattavar uyar aṟivum nākarikamum paṭaittiruntamaiyum, avarkaḷ moḻikaḷ iṉṉum muntiya nāḷ mutaṟkoṇṭē tiruttamuṟṟu mutaṟ coṟkaḷum amaitikaḷum citaiyāmal pātukākkappaṭṭu vantuḷḷamaiyum tāṉ. cittiya iṉattavar nāṭōṭikaḷāyt tiripavarāyiruntamaiyum, āriyar oḻuṅkāṉa kuṭivāḻkkai uṭaiyavarāyiruntamaiyum itaṟku aṭippaṭait tūṇṭutalkaḷāyiruntirukka vēṇṭum. kāraṇam etuvāyiṉum cari, cittiya moḻikaḷiṉ iṉa uṟavukaḷai ārāyum vakaiyil icceytiyai niṉaivil vaittuk koḷḷal vēṇṭum. neṭunāḷ pirikkappaṭṭa cittiya iṉamoḻikaḷiṭaiyē atē iyalpuṭaiya intu - airōppiya - moḻikaḷil kāṇappaṭum nuṇukkamāṉa oṟṟumaikaḷai. etirpārkka muṭiyātu. tiruntā moḻikaḷiṭaiyē, oru moḻiyiṉ vakaittiripukaḷiṭaikkūṭa mika viraivāṉa māṟutalkaḷ ēṟpaṭṭu, māṟupāṭum mikutiyāyviṭṭataṟkāṉa eṭuttuk kāṭṭukaḷaip pēraṟiñar māksmūlar tamatu "moḻiyiyal coṟpoḻivukaḷil” kuṟippiṭukiṟār. itē māksmūlarukku varainta kaṭitamoṉṟil pāṭṭisaṉ pātiriyārum ' oṉṟiṉaiyoṉṟu aṭuttuḷḷa tīvukaḷiraṇṭiṉ moḻikaḷ col tokutiyil muṟṟilum vēṟupaṭṭum, amaippu oṉṟil maṭṭumē oṉṟupaṭṭum kāṇappaṭuvatu iyalpu' eṉṟu itaṉēṭoppak kūṟutal kāṇka.

oru taṉimoḻikku oru moḻiyiṉattuṭaṉ mūṉṟu vakaiyāṉa uṟavumuṟai irukka muṭiyum. (1) nērāṉa kālvaḻi muṟai (2) uṭaṉiyaṅkuṅ tuṇaimoḻi muṟai (3) avviṉattuṭ cārum cārpumuṟai. ivaṟṟuḷ mūṉrum uṟavu muṟaiyaiyē tirāviṭa moḻikaḷ cittiya moḻiyiṉattuṭaṉ koṇṭuḷḷaṉavākak kūṟalām. eṉiṉum, tirāviṭa moḻiyārāycciyāḷarkaḷ itaṉai oppukkoṇṭatākak kūṟa muṭiyātu. tirāviṭa moḻikaḷ peritum intu-airōppiya iṉattaic cērntavaiyē eṉṟu tirāviṭa moḻiyārāyccip pēraṟiñar ṭākṭar pōp karutukiṟar. tamiḻk kaic cuvaṭieṉṉum tam nūliṉ muṉṉuraiyil avar piṉvarumāṟu eḻutiyuḷḷār.

'teṉṉintiya moḻikaḷai evvaḷavukkevvaḷavu āḻntu paṭikkiṉṟōmō avvaḷavukkavvaḷavu avai vaṭamoḻit toṭarpuṭaiyaṉaveṉavum, intu-airōppiya moḻiyiṉattuṭaṉ mikavum neruṅkiya paṇṭait toṭarpu uṭaiyaṉaveṉavum kāṇalām. āṉāl, avai veṟum pākataṅkaḷ eṉṟē, vaṭamoḻic citaivukaḷ eṉṟē kūṟiviṭa muṭiyātu. avai intu-airōppiya moḻiyiṉattuḷ iṭampeṟa vēṇṭiyaṉavē eṉṟum, vaṭamoḻikku iṇaiyāṉa vēṟoru tuṇaimoḻiyiṉ citainta kūṟukaḷē avai eṉṟum, avaiyum vaṭamoḻiyum orē paṇṭai mūla moḻiyiṉ kiḷaikaḷ eṉṟum yām eppoḻutum karuti vantuḷōm. intu-airōppiya moḻiyiṉattaic cērnta kirēkkam, kātik, persiyaṉ mutaliya moḻikaḷuṭaṉ avai neruṅkiya toṭarpuṭaiyaṉa eṉpataṟku uṟutiyāṉa cāṉṟukaḷ pala uḷa ; attakaiya toṭarpu vaṭamoḻikku iruntatākac colvataṟkillai”. Teutonic. kūṟiyatu: 'tutamoḻi ilakkaṇac curukkattiṉ muṉṉuraiyil piṉvarumāṟu kūṟukiṟār: pēraṟiñar kālṭuveluṭaṉ yām peritum otta karuttuṭaiyōm āyiṉum, tirāviṭa moḻikaḷukkum, kelttiya moḻikaḷukkum iṭaiyēyuḷḷa uṟavu iṉṉum naṉku ārāyakkiṭappatoṉṟāl eṇṇukiṉṟōm’ eṉpatē. ittuṟai ārāycci mikavum iṉṟiyamaiyātatē eṉpatil aiyamillai. pōp intu - airōppiya moḻiyuṭaṉē oṟṟumai, uṟavu, iṉaoṟṟumai eṉṟu kūṟuvaṉavellām emakku uṭaṉpāṭē. innūliṉ mutaṟpatippil avaṟṟai yāmē eṭuttukkāṭṭiyum uḷḷēm. āṉāl, av eṭuttukkāṭṭukkaḷiliruntu pōp koṇṭa muṭipu em muṭivāṉa muṭiyuṭaṉ vēṟupaṭukiṟatu. ematu kōṭpāṭu cittiya, intu-airōppiya uṟavukaḷ iraṇṭaṟkum iṭantarupavaiyāka iruppiṉum, intu-airōppiya iṉattaiviṭac cittiya iṉamē tirāviṭak kuḻuvōṭu neruṅkiya uyirnilaiyāṉa toṭarpu uṭaiyatu eṉpatākum. kelttiya uṟavukaḷaippaṟṟi eṭuttukkoṇṭāl, intu - airōppiya moḻikaḷ aṉaittilum immoḻiyiṉamē potuppaṭac cittiya iṉattuṭaṉum, ciṟappāka piṉṉiyak kuḻuvuṭaṉum mika neruṅkiya toṭarpuṭaiyavaiyākat tōṉṟukiṟatu. kelttiyar airōppāvukku varumpōtu aṅkirunta muṉ kuṭikaḷ kelttik moḻiyaiyum piṉṉiṣ moḻiyaiyum pēci vantārkaḷō eṉpatu ārāyattakkatu. mēlum, kelkkiya uṟavukaḷ eṉṟu kūṟappaṭum uṟavukaḷ ciṟappākak kelttiya uṟavukaḷ tāṉā allatu uṇmaiyil cittiya uṟavukaḷ tāṉā eṉpatum oppiṭṭu ārāyattakkatoṉṟām.
